### [1] [Co-adsorption mechanism drives CO oxidation on defective ZnS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00264)
*P. R. A. de Oliveira,C. Codeço,M. G. Menezes,P. Venezuela,F. Stavale,J. A. Boscoboinik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过NAP-XPS和DFT计算发现，在缺陷ZnS单晶表面，CO氧化生成CO₂类中间体的关键因素并非缺陷密度，而是氧覆盖度，证实了氧辅助的CO氧化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽禁带半导体表面反应性依赖于活性位点的产生，但仅在CO氧化中，缺陷的存在不足以驱动反应性。作者希望揭示缺陷ZnS表面CO氧化的真正控制因素。

Method: 结合近环境压力X射线光电子能谱（NAP-XPS）和密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究缺陷ZnS单晶表面的CO氧化反应。

Result: NAP-XPS仅在富氧条件下检测到CO₂类表面中间体；DFT证实了氧辅助的共吸附路径，CO优先与Zn缺陷位附近稳定的吸附氧物种相互作用，形成弱结合的CO₂类结构。

Conclusion: 氧覆盖度而非单纯的缺陷密度是控制缺陷ZnS表面CO₂类中间体形成的关键因素，缺陷ZnS可作为研究非氧化物半导体氧辅助表面化学的模型平台。

Abstract: Reactivity on wide-bandgap semiconductor surfaces relies critically on the generation of active sites. In the case of CO oxidation, however, the mere presence of defects is insufficient to drive reactivity. Here, we investigate CO oxidation on a defective ZnS single-crystal surface by combining near ambient pressure X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (NAP-XPS) and density functional theory (DFT) calculations. NAP-XPS measurements reveal CO$_2$-like surface intermediates only under oxygen-rich conditions, consistent with oxygen-assisted CO oxidation. DFT calculations support an oxygen-assisted co-adsorption pathway in which CO interacts preferentially with adsorbed oxygen species stabilized near Zn-deficient sites, forming weakly bound CO$_2$-like structures. These results identify oxygen coverage, rather than defect density alone, as the key factor controlling CO$_2$-like intermediate formation on defective ZnS and establish defective ZnS as a model platform for studying oxygen-assisted surface chemistry on non-oxide semiconductors.

</details>


### [2] [Robust control over polar skyrmion bubble density with a combined optical and electrical approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00398)
*Lingyuan Gao,Laurent Bellaiche*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算证明，在直流电场辅助下，具有空间非均匀场分布的扭曲光可以稳健地调控铁电超薄膜中极性斯格明子气泡的密度（10²~10⁴ bit/μm²），并可控制其成核速率，为下一代超高密度存储技术提供了关键基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性斯格明子气泡因其独特的拓扑结构和电学响应，被认为是非易失性存储器件中有前景的候选对象。在存储器件中，信息记录密度直接正比于气泡密度，因此需要精确控制气泡的成核过程。

Method: 采用基于第一性原理的计算方法，在直流电场辅助下，利用具有空间非均匀场分布的扭曲光对铁电超薄膜进行调控。

Result: 扭曲光可以稳健地将极性斯格明子气泡密度调控在10²~10⁴ bit/μm²范围内。通过调节直流和光场强度以及光束半径，还可以有效控制气泡的成核速率（表征气泡的产生和湮灭速度）。

Conclusion: 铁电纳米薄膜对光场和电场具有独特的响应，这对于将极性斯格明子气泡应用于下一代超高密度存储技术至关重要。

Abstract: Polar skyrmion bubbles are nanoscale ferroelectric domain configurations with swirling polarization textures, and often emerge in ferroelectric oxide systems. Owing to their inhomogeneous polarization patterns, which endow them with distinct topologies and electrical responses from homogeneous monodomains, polar skyrmion bubbles are envisaged to be promising candidates for non-volatile memory devices. In such device, the recorded information density is directly proportional to the density of bubbles, underscoring the need for precise control over bubble nucleation. Here, using first-principles-based calculations, we demonstrate that when assisted with a DC electric field, twisted light, which has a spatially inhomogeneous field pattern, can robustly tune the density of polar skyrmion bubbles in ferroelectric ultrathin films between $10^2\sim 10^4 \rm{bit}/μm^2$. Moreover, by modulating DC and optical field strengths together with the beam radius, the nucleation rate, which characterizes the creation and annihilation speed of polar skyrmion bubbles, can also be well controlled. These findings highlight the unique response of ferroelectric nanofilms to optical and electric fields, which is crucial for employing polar skyrmion bubbles in the next-generation of ultrahigh-density memory technologies.

</details>


### [3] [Microscopic origin of polytype-dependent melting in SiC revealed by machine-learning molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00403)
*Ljiljana Stojanović,Samuel J. Magorrian,Lara Kabalan,Richard N. White,Fabian L. Thiemann,Viktor Zólyomi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合机器学习分子动力学与有限温度声子分析，发现SiC多型体的高温稳定性排序为3C > 2H > 9R，其中长周期9R的不稳定性源于低频横向声学剪切模的软化，表明共价材料的高温稳定性不仅取决于局域键强，还受堆叠序列依赖的横向动力学控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体结构如何影响高温稳定性是材料建模与设计中的关键挑战，SiC因其多样的多型体（局域四面体键合相似但长程堆叠几何不同）成为研究这一问题的理想体系。

Method: 结合相共存机器学习分子动力学与有限温度声子分析，使用微调的MACE原子间势能准确描述多种SiC多型体的晶态、高温及无序构型。

Result: 明确了相对稳定性排序为3C > 2H > 9R；熔融通过形成短C-C接触和富碳局域区域开始，随后逐渐失去四面体Si-C连接；9R的不稳定性归因于与双层滑移相关的低频横向声学剪切模，这些模在0 K已存在并在高温下进一步软化。

Conclusion: 多型共价材料的高温稳定性不仅受局域键合强度调控，还依赖于堆叠序列依赖的横向动力学行为。

Abstract: Predicting how crystal structure influences high-temperature stability remains a key challenge in materials modelling and design. Silicon carbide (SiC), one of the most thermally and chemically stable materials known, provides an ideal system for studying this problem because its many polytypes preserve similar local tetrahedral bonding while differing in long-range stacking geometry. Here, we combine phase-coexistence machine-learning molecular dynamics with finite-temperature phonon analysis, enabled by a fine-tuned MACE interatomic potential that accurately describes crystalline, high-temperature, and disordered configurations across multiple SiC polytypes. We identify a clear relative stability ordering, 3C > 2H > 9R, reflected consistently in structural disordering, interlayer sliding, and finite-temperature phonon spectra. Across all polytypes, melting initiates through the formation of short C-C contacts and carbon-rich local regions, followed by a progressive loss of tetrahedral Si-C connectivity. The reduced stability of the long-period 9R polytype is traced to low-frequency transverse-acoustic shear modes associated with relative bilayer sliding, which are already present in the 0 K phonon spectra and soften further at high temperature. These modes generate larger lateral bilayer displacements, linking enhanced interlayer sliding to local chemical disordering and ultimately melting. More broadly, our results show that high-temperature stability in polytypic covalent materials is governed not only by local bond strength, but also by stacking-dependent transverse dynamics.

</details>


### [4] [Giant Thermal-Conductivity Enhancement from Pseudo-Angular Momentum Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00546)
*Tingting Wang,Dabao Zha,Hao Chen,Jiangbin Gong,Lifa Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现赝角动量（PAM）守恒对手性声子热输运施加了隐藏的选择定则，通过将PAM投影引入第一性原理玻尔兹曼输运方程，可在不改变谱和力常数的情况下排除约三分之二的动力学允许三声子过程，使螺旋碲的晶格热导率在300K时提升5.30倍（直至400K仍保持5倍以上），在手性晶体基准中也能将计算热导率提升约24%，从而建立了PAM守恒作为手性声子热输运的非谐选择原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决赝角动量（PAM）是否也约束热平衡下有限波矢声子-声子散射这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 通过旋转或螺旋本征相位守恒推导出立方非谐顶点的PAM余数定则，并将其作为投影算符引入第一性原理玻尔兹曼输运方程中。

Result: 在螺旋碲中，该投影移除了约三分之二的运动学允许三声子过程，抑制了电阻性Umklapp弛豫，使300K下晶格热导率提升5.30倍（400K仍保持5倍以上）；在手性晶体基准中，热导率提升约24%，与实验的偏差相当。

Conclusion: 建立了PAM守恒作为手性声子热输运的非谐选择原理，并指出对称性分辨的PAM守恒是预测和调控手性晶体及纳米声子学中热导率的新途径。

Abstract: Pseudo-angular momentum (PAM) underlies optical selection rules for chiral phonons, but whether it also constrains thermally populated finite-q phonon-phonon scattering has remained unresolved. We show that rotational or screw eigenphase conservation imposes a PAM residue rule on cubic anharmonic vertices, revealing a hidden selection rule for heat transport. In screw-symmetric helical Te, an exact platform, implementing this rule as a projector in first-principles Boltzmann transport leaves spectra and force constants unchanged but removes roughly two thirds of kinematically allowed triplets, suppresses resistive Umklapp relaxation, and enhances lattice thermal conductivity by a factor of 5.30 at 300 K, remaining above fivefold up to 400 K. A bulk chiral-crystal benchmark further shows that explicit eigenphase organization can increase the calculated lattice thermal conductivity by about 24%, comparable to the reported first-principles underestimation of experiment. These results establish PAM conservation as an anharmonic selection principle for chiral-phonon heat transport and identify symmetry-resolved PAM conservation as a route to predicting and controlling thermal conductivity in chiral crystals and nanoscale phononics.

</details>


### [5] [Impact of Cu-Mn ratio on Structure and Defects in Layered Multiferroic Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00742)
*Sai Venkata Gayathri Ayyagari,Boyang Zheng,Sreekant Anil,Subrata Ghosh,Yuxi Zhang,Yu Liu,Chandan De,Ke Wang,Jeffrey Shallenberger,Weiwei Xie,Vincent H. Crespi,Zhiqiang Mao,Nasim Alem*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过电子显微镜和X射线衍射研究了Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3晶体中富Cu和缺Cu样品的纳米尺度结构缺陷差异，发现缺Cu晶体存在大量堆垛层错，而富Cu晶体则出现针状沉淀和环状特征，并结合DFT计算解释了这些结构差异对多铁性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3存在强磁电耦合效应且Mn/Cu比例变化会影响其光学、电子和磁学响应，但纳米尺度结构和缺陷对宏观性能的影响机制尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 采用扫描/透射电子显微镜和单晶X射线衍射对富Cu和缺Cu的Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3晶体进行结构表征，并结合密度泛函理论计算进行验证。

Result: 缺Cu晶体存在大量堆垛层错，与Mn/Cu化学不均匀性和Te堆垛变化相关；富Cu晶体层错较少，但出现针状沉淀和环状特征等局部结构变化。DFT计算确认富Cu结构比缺Cu结构更具极性。

Conclusion: 化学组成的细微变化显著影响纳米尺度结构、缺陷分布和功能性质，为设计具有定制性能的多铁材料提供了指导。

Abstract: Multiferroic materials exhibit the coexistence of magnetic and ferroelectric order, enabling control of magnetism through electric fields and vice versa. These properties make them attractive for spintronic and memory device applications. Recent studies on Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3 (0.04 \leq x \leq 0.26; 0.03 \leq y \leq 0.15) have revealed strong magnetoelectric coupling, with variations in Mn-to-Cu concentration leading to variations in optical, electronic, and magnetic responses. Despite these findings, the influence of nanoscale structure and defects on the observed properties remains poorly understood. In this study, we investigate the structure and nanoscale defects in Cu-deficient Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3 (Cu:Mn ratio <1, i.e., with 0.04 \leq x \leq 0.26 and 0.03 \leq y \leq 0.15) and Cu-rich Cu1+xMn1-ySiTe3 (Cu:Mn ratio >1, i.e., with 0.04 \leq x \leq 0.3 and 0.13 \leq y \leq 0.31) crystals using scanning/transmission electron microscopy and single-crystal X-ray diffraction. Cu-deficient crystals exhibit extensive stacking faults correlated with chemical inhomogeneity between Mn and Cu, along with variations in Te stacking. In contrast, Cu-rich crystals show fewer stacking faults but contain other local structural variations, such as needle-shaped precipitates and loop-like features. These distinct local structural features between Cu-rich and Cu-deficient crystals can be correlated to variations in their observed properties. Complementary density functional theory calculations confirm that the Cu-rich structure is more polar than the Cu-deficient structure. Overall, this study provides a comprehensive understanding of how subtle changes in chemistry influence the nanoscale structure, defect distribution, and functional properties in Cu1-xMn1+ySiTe3, offering guidance for designing multiferroic materials with tailored performance.

</details>


### [6] [Thermodynamic origin of medium-entropy stabilization in multicomponent rock-salt oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00753)
*Supriya Ghosal,Swapan Pati,Ashutosh Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理热力学框架研究了含2-5种阳离子的岩盐氧化物的相稳定性，发现构型熵（ΔS_conf）不是相稳定性的普适描述符，三、四、五阳离子体系在高温下可被熵驱动稳定，表明单相岩盐氧化物不局限于传统高熵极限，中熵组分在合适热力学条件下也能稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵氧化物通常需要五种等摩尔阳离子实现高构型熵（ΔS_conf≥1.61R），但近期实验发现中熵组分也可表现出熵稳定的岩盐相，引发关于相稳定所需最小熵的关键问题。本文旨在系统探究从二元到五元阳离子体系中熵对相稳定性的作用机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理热力学框架，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、MCSQS结构建模以及有限温度吉布斯自由能分析，定量计算了混合焓（ΔH_mix）、构型熵（ΔS_conf）、振动熵（ΔS_vib）和电子熵（ΔS_elec）对相稳定性的贡献。

Result: ΔS_conf单独不能作为相稳定性的普适描述符；二元阳离子体系为焓稳定，而三元、四元和五元阳离子体系在高温下因熵驱动的吉布斯自由能降低而变得热力学稳定。

Conclusion: 单相岩盐氧化物不限于传统高熵极限（五阳离子等摩尔比），中熵组分在合适热力学条件下同样可实现熵稳定，拓展了熵稳定氧化物的组分设计空间。

Abstract: High entropy oxides are commonly associated with high configurational entropy ($ΔS_{conf}\geq$ 1.61R) corresponding to five equimolar cations occupying a crystallographic sublattice. However, recent experimental observations indicate that medium-entropy compositions may also exhibit entropy-stabilized rock-salt phases, raising an important question regarding the minimum entropy required for phase stabilization. In this work, we employ a first-principles thermodynamic framework to investigate the stability of rock-salt oxides containing two to five principal cations components analogous to (Ni$_{0.8}$Cu$_{0.2}$)O, (Ni$_{0.6}$Cu$_{0.2}$Zn$_{0.2}$)O, (Ni$_{0.4}$Cu$_{0.2}$Zn$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$)O, (Ni$_{0.2}$Cu$_{0.2}$Zn$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Mg$_{0.2}$)O. Density functional theory, MCSQS-based structural modeling, and finite-temperature Gibbs free-energy analysis are combined to quantify the roles of enthalpy mixing ($ΔH_{mix}$), configurational ($ΔS_{conf}$), vibrational ($ΔS_{vib}$), and electronic contributions towards ($ΔS_{elec}$) entropy change in governing phase stability. The results show that $ΔS_{conf}$ alone is not a universal descriptor of phase stability. While the two-cation system is enthalpy-stabilized but three-, four- and five-cation systems become thermodynamically stable at high-temperature due to entropy-driven reduction of the Gibbs free energy. These findings demonstrate that single-phase rock-salt oxides are not restricted to the conventional high-entropy limit and that medium-entropy compositions can also be stabilized under suitable thermodynamic conditions.

</details>


### [7] [Autonomous scanning electrochemical cell microscopy enables rapid exploration of large compositionally complex material spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00779)
*Felix Thelen,Moonjoo Kim,Geovane Arruda de Oliveira,Jan Lukas Buergel,Wolfgang Schuhmann,Alfred Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自主扫描电化学池显微镜（SECCM）的超高通量平台，结合主动学习和自动库交换，快速建立成分复杂固溶体（CCSS）电催化剂的组成-活性关系。在Au-Ir-Rh体系中，仅测量15%的区域即可准确预测全组成空间的析氢反应活性，发现Au30Ir20Rh50和Au10Ir35Rh55具有最高活性（速率常数约0.012 cm/s），展示了元素混合的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成分复杂固溶体（高熵合金）在电催化中具有巨大潜力，但可能的成分组合存在"组合爆炸"问题，传统筛选方法效率极低，严重制约了活性CCSS电催化剂的发现。

Method: 开发了自主SECCM系统，集成主动学习算法（可学习完整伏安图而非单一指标）和自动库交换功能，实现对多个薄膜CCSS材料库的超高通量、大规模组成-电催化活性关系筛选。

Result: 在Au-Ir-Rh三元体系中，仅测量全部966个区域的15%即可准确预测全成分空间的析氢反应活性趋势；鉴定出Au30Ir20Rh50和Au10Ir35Rh55为最优成分，标准速率常数约0.012 cm/s。

Conclusion: 该自主机器人SECCM平台具有广泛适用性，可应用于多种电催化反应，为加速CCSS电催化剂发现与优化提供了通用方法。

Abstract: Alloying is a central strategy in electrocatalysis, enabling fine-tuning of electronic structure. In particular, compositionally complex solid solutions (CCSS) often called high-entropy alloys are of high interest as they allow active site design. However, the "combinatorial explosion" in the number of possible compositions poses a critical bottleneck for the discovery of active CCSS electrocatalysts. We present an autonomous scanning electrochemical cell microscopy (SECCM) system for ultrahigh-throughput and large-scale CCSS activity screening. The platform rapidly establishes composition-electrocatalytic activity relationships for large compositional spaces across multiple thin-film CCSS materials libraries via active learning and automated library exchange. Embedding analytical expressions of voltammetry in the algorithm enables the learning of whole voltammograms rather than a single selected metric. As a demonstration, we investigated hydrogen evolution reaction (HER) activities of Au-Ir-Rh, where Ir and Rh exhibit strong metal-hydrogen binding and Au exhibits relatively weak binding as derived from the HER volcano plot. The composition-activity trend was accurately predicted after measuring only 15% of all 966 measurement areas. Au30Ir20Rh50 and Au10Ir35Rh55 exhibit highest activities with standard rate constants of about 0.012 cm/s, demonstrating positive synergistic contributions from elemental mixing. The autonomous robotic SECCM platform is broadly applicable to a wide range of electrocatalytic reactions, providing a general pathway for accelerating CCSS electrocatalyst discovery and optimization.

</details>


### [8] [Compressibility and High-Pressure Structure of CaMg$_2$Bi$_2$ and YbMg$_2$Bi$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00785)
*Mario Calderón-Cueva,Allison Pease,Cheng Peng,Wanyue Peng,Megan Rylko,Weiwei Xie,Susannah M. Dorfman,Alexandra Zevalkink*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了CaMg₂Bi₂和YbMg₂Bi₂在高压下的结构行为，发现两者在超过9.6 GPa和8.7 GPa时发生相变，新高压相为空间群C2/m的扭曲CaAl₂Si₂结构，并揭示了各向异性压缩性主要源于八面体A-Bi键的柔顺性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CaAl₂Si₂结构类型的AM₂X₂化合物在量子拓扑和热电材料领域备受关注，但此前仅在少数化合物（如Mg₃Sb₂、CaMn₂Bi₂等）中发现高压相变，CaMg₂Bi₂和YbMg₂Bi₂的高压行为尚不明确。

Method: 采用熔融金属助熔剂法合成晶体，利用金刚石对顶砧施加最高20 GPa的压力，通过单晶同步辐射X射线衍射进行结构表征。

Result: 发现CaMg₂Bi₂和YbMg₂Bi₂分别在9.6 GPa和8.7 GPa以上发生结构相变，新高压相为单斜C2/m空间群，且压缩各向异性主要由八面体配位的A-Bi键贡献。

Conclusion: 两类化合物的高压相变行为及新相结构不同于相关化合物（如CaMn₂Bi₂），后者M位为四方锥配位，揭示了CaAl₂Si₂结构类型在高压下的结构多样性。

Abstract: Compounds with the formula $AM_2X_2$ in the CaAl$_2$Si$_2$ structure type have garnered increasing interest across various solid-state research domains, such as quantum topological and thermoelectric materials. Prior studies have identified high-pressure phase transitions in several compounds, including Mg$_3$Sb$_2$, Mg$_3$Bi$_2$, CaMn$_2$Bi$_2$, and SrAl$_2$Si$_2$. In this study, we investigate the structural behavior of CaMg$_2$Bi$_2$ and YbMg$_2$Bi$_2$ under varying pressure conditions. We synthesized crystals using the molten metal flux method and examined them through single-crystal synchrotron X-ray diffraction, employing diamond anvil cells to exert pressures up to 20 GPa. Our analysis reveals insights into the anisotropic compressibility of these materials, highlighting the more compressible and flexible octahedral $A$-Bi bonds as the primary contributors to this anisotropy. Moreover, we observed a phase transition in both CaMg$_2$Bi$_2$ and YbMg$_2$Bi$_2$ at pressures above 9.6 GPa and 8.7 GPa, respectively. The newly identified high-pressure phase exhibits a distortion of the original CaAl$_2$Si$_2$ structure with space group $C2/m$. The resulting framework combines structured experimental descriptors, semantic literature retrieval, and mechanistic interpretation to generate confidence-aware recommendations for optimal defect-engineering conditions. Multi-hydrogen analysis demonstrates that trapping behavior is concentration-dependent; increasing hydrogen concentration progressively reduces the available free volume and increases local lattice distortion. An optimization scheme iteratively adjusts an uniform interstitial chemical potential across the mapped microstructure until the integrated local concentration converges with an independently measured bulk value. Abstract: Dielectric properties of Nb-doped (~1.5 at%) rutile single crystals were studied in the 10-300 K temperature range (at frequencies below the MHz range down to 0.3 K) in a broad frequency range, up to terahertz and infrared range, to understand the origin of its giant permittivity. The results were fitted, modelled and compared with those of the undoped rutile crystal measured in the terahertz and infrared ranges. The primary effect originates from the near-electrode depletion layer of lower conductivity compared to the bulk (surface barrier-layer capacitor effect), which causes a strong thermally activated relaxation in the MHz dielectric spectra. In the higher frequency range, the main difference between doped and undoped crystals is the presence of an overdamped microwave excitation (central mode) in the doped crystal for both polarizations, persisting down to 10 K and not thermally activated. This accounts for the previously reported permittivity increase, even at 2 K - where all lower-frequency relaxations are frozen - compared to undoped crystals. It also explains why our low-frequency permittivity at 0.3K exceeds the THz value. The origin of this excitation remains unclear and requires further investigations. Doping affects polar phonons only by slightly increasing their damping.

Above $P_\text{c}$ a significant redistribution of spectral weight occurs in the optical conductivity spectrum for both materials. Abstract: Altermagnetism and topological insulators represent two of the most transformative frontiers in modern condensed matter physics, spintronics, and quantum information science. Abstract: Nonlinear optical (NLO) responses in two-dimensional quantum-confined systems are typically described within bulk-based frameworks as macroscopic spatial averages. We reveal a layer-resolved organization into skin, weak-skin, and hidden effects governed by local symmetry and stacking order. First-principles calculations for both nonmagnetic and spin-polarized systems confirm our predictions, demonstrating that stacking alone suffices to dramatically reshape both the spatial pattern and magnitude of the NLO response, a phenomenon not explainable within standard bulk theory. Conclusion: 逆向材料设计虽取得显著进展，但仍面临代理模型利用、多样性崩塌、分布偏移和稳定性-可合成性差距等系统性问题，需建立基于有效性、新颖性、独特性、稳定性和成本的分阶段评估实践以提升发现质量。

Abstract: Inverse materials design is shifting materials discovery from forward prediction to targeted proposal of candidates that satisfy objectives under physical constraints. Here, we review recent advances in generative crystal structure modeling, multimodal learning, and closed-loop design pipelines for crystalline solids. We survey how modern generators learn chemical-structural priors from large databases to enable controllable sampling of periodic structures, and compare leading model classes including variational autoencoders, normalizing flows, autoregressive formulations, and diffusion models. Particular attention is given to how feasibility constraints and physical priors are enforced across the workflow, through representation choices, training objectives, sampling-time guidance, and post-generation screening and relaxation. We also discuss how multimodal learning fuses diverse materials modalities, including crystal structures, thermodynamic, electronic information, microscopy, spectroscopy, processing context, and scientific text, to construct a more universal, transferable representation of chemical space. In addition, diverse inverse-design strategies are examined, particularly those that integrate conditional generation with latent optimization, Bayesian optimization, reinforcement learning, and active learning. Finally, we highlight recurring failure modes, such as surrogate exploitation, diversity collapse, distribution shift, and the stability-synthesizability gap, and outline discovery-grade evaluation practices based on staged reporting of validity, novelty, uniqueness, stability, and cost.

</details>

Abstract: Weyl semimetals and altermagnets represent two distinct classes of quantum materials exhibiting nontrivial topological and magnetic order, respectively. Here we report the realization of a Weyl nodal-loop altermagnet in Cr$_7$Se$_8$, combining neutron diffraction and first-principles calculations. The hexagonal system hosts a coplanar $120^\circ$ compensated magnetic order on a triangular lattice, which breaks inversion-time-reversal and translation-time-reversal symmetries simultaneously while preserving a crystalline mirror plane. The resulting electronic structure features linearly dispersing nodal loops close to the Fermi level ($E_F$) confined to the mirror-invariant $k_z=0$ plane. Along high-symmetry directions, the crossings near $E_F$ form Dirac-like fourfold degeneracies in the absence of spin-orbit coupling; at generic momenta, these crossings split into twofold and form continuous Weyl-like nodal loops protected by mirror symmetry. The momentum-dependent spin polarization exhibits an $f$-wave-like pattern characteristic of odd-parity altermagnets.

Abstract: The production of hydrogen through water splitting in a photoelectrochemical cell suffers from an overpotential that limits the efficiencies. Conclusion: DPA4在Matbench Discovery上建立了新的精度-成本帕累托前沿，并成为未来多任务大型原子模型（LAM）预训练的有力候选骨干架构。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials now approach quantum-mechanical accuracy on standard benchmarks, but the training cost of the most expressive equivariant architectures has become a serious bottleneck. We introduce DPA4, an SE(3)-equivariant interatomic-potential architecture with an EMFA (Edge-conditioned, Multi-Focus, Attention) SO(2)-equivariant convolution that combines a low-rank edge-node SO(2)-equivariant product, a multi-focus design for message nonlinearity, and envelope-gated attention for message aggregation. A Lebedev-grid projection further preserves SO(3)-equivariance in the nonlinearity to machine precision. A compiler-friendly conservative energy-gradient training path provides up to $\sim$3 times wall-clock speedup under torch.compile. On the compliant Matbench Discovery benchmark, DPA4-Pro attains the best Combined Performance Score (CPS) on the leaderboard, while the 2.76M-parameter DPA4-Air exceeds the accuracy of the 30.1M-parameter eSEN-30M-MP baseline with 10.9$\times$ fewer parameters and 42.9$\times$ less training compute. On SPICE-MACE-OFF, the 5.4M-parameter DPA4-Plus lowers the aggregate molecular energy and force errors of the 6.5M-parameter eSEN baseline by 29% and 30%, while the 2.7M-parameter DPA4-Air still surpasses that baseline with $\sim$2.4$\times$ fewer parameters. Together these results place DPA4 on a new accuracy-cost Pareto frontier on Matbench Discovery and position it as a strong candidate backbone for future multi-task large atomistic model (LAM) pretraining.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该随机方法在保证精度的前提下显著降低了内存需求，为正电子-分子体系的关联效应计算提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: A diagrammatic Monte Carlo evaluation of the ladder series contributions to the correlation potential (self energy) of a positron in the field of a molecule is presented. The $GW$@TDHF, virtual-positronium ($T$-matrix), and positron-hole Goldstone ladder series contributions are stochastically sampled order-by-order within the Tamm-Dancoff approximation, which is exact for the latter two classes, with Ces{á}ro-Riesz resummation used to extrapolate to infinite order. Gaussian bases are employed and Coulomb matrix elements are represented via density fitting, with the three centre integrals the largest arrays required to be stored in memory. The stochastic approach thus realizes a reduction in memory of the largest arrays required on the order of the number of molecular orbitals in the basis $N\sim$10$^2$--10$^3$ compared to the exact deterministic solution of Bethe-Salpeter equations [J. Hofierka, B. Cunningham, C. M. Rawlins, C. H. Patterson and D. G. Green, Nature {\bf 606}, {688} (2022)]. Benchmark results for lithium hydride show quantitative agreement with exact diagonalisation, notably demonstrating the successful stochastic summation of the virtual-positronium infinite electron-positron ladder series.

</details>

### [30] [Emergent Collaborative Deliberation in Multi-Model AI Systems: A BFT-Derived Protocol for Epistemic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00005)
*VD Doske*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Consilium协议是一种基于拜占庭容错的架构，通过为语言模型分配认知人格并引入样本内/样本外验证框架，将模型间分歧视为认知信号而非错误。实验表明：认知人格而非底层模型决定认知行为（低价模型与高价模型分析质量相当）；RLHF对齐训练在特定领域造成认知盲点；协议本身无方向性偏差；样本外证据检索验证了239个主张并发现167个盲点。全部成本仅217美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在面对多模型分歧时通常将其视为错误并寻求消歧，未能充分利用分歧作为认知信号的价值。此外，RLHF对齐训练可能引入系统性偏差，需要一种结构化的方法对多模型推理进行鲁棒整合和偏差检测。

Method: 采用拜占庭容错协议架构，为每个语言模型分配独立的认知人格（将模型身份与推理方式解耦），并引入量化金融领域的样本内/样本外验证框架，区分基于训练数据的共识与基于经验证据的结论。在1,478轮会话、32个主题、10个领域类别上进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 认知人格决定认知行为，低成本模型（0.0002美元/批）与分析前沿模型（10.69美元）质量相当；(2) RLHF训练在争议性政策主题上产生12.3个百分点的对抗性挑战缺失，AI安全主题呈现11.6%的非对称偏差；(3) 协议自身无方向性偏差（移民Δ=2.3%，可再生能源Δ=1.2%）；(4) 样本外检索100%验证239个主张，发现167个训练数据不可见的盲点。运行可重复性标准差±2.2%，总成本217美元。

Conclusion: Consilium协议提供了一种低成本、高可靠性的多模型AI推理结构化方法，能有效检测RLHF引入的认知盲点，并将模型分歧转化为有价值的认知信号。协议规范以MIT许可证开源发布，支持独立验证。

Abstract: We present the Consilium Protocol, a Byzantine Fault Tolerance-derived architecture for structured multi-model AI deliberation that treats inter-model disagreement as epistemic signal rather than error. The protocol assigns engineered cognitive personas to language models -- separating what a model is from how it reasons -- and introduces an In-Sample/Out-of-Sample validation framework adapted from quantitative finance to distinguish training-data consensus from empirically grounded conclusions. Across 1,478 deliberation sessions spanning 32 topics in 10 domain categories, we demonstrate that (1) the cognitive persona, not the underlying model, determines epistemic behavior: free edge-inference models costing 0.0002 USD per batch produced comparable analytical output to frontier models costing 10.69 USD; (2) RLHF alignment training creates measurable, domain-specific epistemic blind spots -- contested policy topics exhibit 12.3 percentage points less adversarial challenge than settled science topics, and AI safety topics show asymmetric bias ($Δ$=11.6%) where models challenge claims that AI is dangerous far more vigorously than claims that AI risk is overstated; (3) the protocol exhibits no directional bias of its own (immigration $Δ$=2.3%, renewables $Δ$=1.2%); and (4) out-of-sample evidence retrieval validated 239 claims with 100% evidence retrieval and surfaced 167 blind-spot discoveries invisible to training-data deliberation. Run-to-run reproducibility across randomized model$\times$persona assignments averages $\pm$2.2% standard deviation. Total cost for the complete battery including all overhead: 217 USD. We release the protocol specification under MIT license to enable independent verification.

</details>


### [31] [Optimal Transport-based Permutation-Invariant Bayesian Optimization of Offshore Wind Farm Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00009)
*Antonio Candelieri,Laurens Bliek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输理论的排列不变贝叶斯优化方法（PIBO），用于解决具有排列对称性的布局优化问题（如海上风电场布局），相比传统贝叶斯优化，PIBO能获得更优布局并将计算时间减少约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化无法利用目标问题中存在的对称性（例如风电场中风机排列顺序不影响目标函数值），导致优化效率低下。

Method: 基于最优传输理论，提出排列不变贝叶斯优化（PIBO），将布局优化问题中的排列对称性纳入考虑，区别于点云优化（顺序相关）。

Result: 相比标准贝叶斯优化，PIBO能获得更优的风电场布局方案，同时计算时间减少约一半。

Conclusion: PIBO有效利用了布局问题的排列不变性，在提升优化质量和计算效率方面均有显著优势。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely and successfully adopted for solving optimization problems having an expensive-to-evaluate, black-box, and non-convex objective function. However, the vanilla BO algorithm is not able to exploit possible symmetries characterizing the target problem. An intuitive case is given by optimal location problems, whose decision variables refer to a finite set of points within a continuous space, with the order of points not affecting the value of the objective function. We refer to this setting as optimization over layouts to distinguish from optimization over point-clouds where, instead, the order of points counts. As an instance of optimization over layouts we consider a real-life industrial-relevant application, that is the optimization of the layout of an offshore wind farm: given identical wind turbines, switching any pair of them has not any effect on the annual energy production. Based on Optimal Transport theory, we propose a Permutation-Invariant BO approach, namely PIBO, proved to provide better wind farm layouts when compared to the vanilla BO approach while cutting computation time roughly in half.

</details>


### [32] [Absorbing Complexity: An Interaction-Native Knowledge Harness for Financial LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01886)
*Ailiya Borjigin,Igor Stadnyk,Ben Bilski,Maksym Chikita,Dmytro Kyrylenko,Sofiia Pidturkina,Julia Stadnyk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互原生知识框架(InKH)，一种金融LLM Agent架构，通过被动知识注入、时序图记忆、Wiki审计面等机制将复杂性吸收到系统中而非转移给用户。在合成基准测试中，InKH在900ms延迟下达到0.815的平均任务质量，相比基线降低了82.95%延迟、82.29% token成本和96.58%过时知识使用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI Agent迫使用户反复陈述目标、风险偏好、投资组合上下文和过往判断，Agent却"回答、检索、行动、遗忘"，导致延迟、重复错误、弱可审计性和不安全决策。本文旨在将复杂性从用户侧转移到系统侧。

Method: InKH架构包含四个核心机制：(1)被动知识注入——在主模型步骤前构建有界工作上下文缓冲区；(2)时序图记忆——实现低延迟检索；(3)Wiki审计面——提供人类可读的治理记录；(4)背景提取——引入成熟度、衰减和写入时失效机制，管理知识生命周期。

Result: 在24个随机种子、4轮、每轮80集、6个基线的合成基准测试中，共46,080次评估。InKH达到0.815任务质量（900ms延迟）。相比Agent驱动的Wiki-walk记忆：延迟降低82.95%，token成本降低82.29%，过时知识使用降低96.58%，质量提升0.108，可追溯性提升0.461。相比无失效机制的时序图系统：质量提升0.050，过时记忆使用降低96.58%。

Conclusion: 金融AI的采纳发生在系统吸收复杂性而非将其转移给用户时。基准测试验证的是架构层面的行为表现，而非实盘交易性能。

Abstract: Financial AI agents often fail for a simple reason: they make users carry the complexity. A user must repeatedly restate goals, risk preferences, portfolio context, past judgments, and shifting market assumptions, while the agent answers, retrieves, acts, and forgets. In finance, this is not just inconvenient. In tasks such as market analysis, copy-trading review, and trade preparation, forgotten context and stale memory can create latency, repeated errors, weak auditability, and unsafe decisions.
  We propose the interaction-native knowledge harness (InKH), an architecture for financial LLM agents that absorbs complexity into the system. InKH converts user, market, portfolio, and tool events into structured operational knowledge. It uses passive knowledge injection to assemble a bounded working context buffer before the main model step, temporal graph memory for low-latency retrieval, a wiki audit surface for human-readable governance, and background extraction with maturity, decay, and write-time invalidation.
  We evaluate InKH on a reproducible controlled synthetic benchmark with 24 random seeds, 4 rounds, 80 episodes per round, and 6 baselines, producing 46,080 baseline-conditioned evaluations. InKH achieves mean task quality of 0.815 at 900 ms latency. Compared with agent-driven wiki-walk memory, it reduces latency by 82.95 percent, token cost by 82.29 percent, and stale-knowledge usage by 96.58 percent, while improving quality by 0.108 and traceability by 0.461. Compared with a temporal-graph system without invalidation, it improves quality by 0.050 and reduces stale-memory usage by 96.58 percent with comparable serving cost.
  The results support a design thesis for financial AI: adoption happens when complexity is absorbed by the system rather than transferred to the user. The benchmark validates architecture-level behavior, not live trading performance.

</details>


### [33] [On Wednesdays, We Ask Questions: Optimizing "Active Listening" in Automated Legal Triage and Referral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00272)
*Quinten Steenhuis,Jacqueline Harvey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FETCH分类器使用低成本LLM集成生成追问问题以优化法律问题匹配。研究发现低成本模型分类表现良好但生成高质量问题需要高成本模型（如GPT-5）；提出法律接案分类问题评估标准；发现LLM评判与人类评分存在分歧；单一大模型可提升问题质量与分类准确性；部分法律领域（如家庭暴力）的事实获取不均，提示需设置专项筛查机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用低成本LLM集成在法律咨询场景中生成追问问题以优化匹配结果，并评估不同成本模型的性能差异。

Method: 采用专家律师与LLM联合评估方法；提出法律接案分类问题评估标准（rubric）；对比低成本LLM与高成本模型（GPT-5）在问题生成质量上的差异；采用LLM-as-judge与人工评分进行分歧分析。

Result: 低成本LLM分类性能良好但问题生成质量不足；仅靠提示工程无法有效提升问题质量；LLM评判与人类评分存在显著分歧；引入GPT-5可显著提升问题质量与分类准确性；部分法律类别（如家庭暴力）存在事实获取不均问题。

Conclusion: 生成高质量法律咨询追问问题需要高成本模型；提出法律接案分类问题评估标准；需针对特定法律领域（如家庭暴力）设置专项筛查机制以弥补通用模型的不均衡表现。

Abstract: The FETCH classifier generates follow-up questions to help refine the best match for the applicant's legal problem, using a low-cost ensemble of LLMs. In this paper, we describe an expert attorney and LLM-assisted evaluation of the follow-up question approach in FETCH and show that while low-cost LLMs perform well at classification tasks, generating high-quality plain-language questions in this setting appears to require a more sophisticated and higher-cost model. Through discussion with legal intake workers, we propose a rubric for the evaluation of legal intake classification questions, and we find that prompt engineering alone is not enough to improve question quality for intake purposes. We also find that LLM-as-judge and human ratings diverge. We demonstrate that with the addition of a single high-cost model, GPT-5, the classifier can elicit relevant information from applicants for legal help, and that the questions lead to more accurate performance at classification tasks. We also find uneven fact elicitation across different categories, including domestic violence, at odds with family law screening protocols, suggesting the value of including dedicated screening panels for certain areas of law.

</details>


### [34] [Model-Native Computing Architecture: Envisioning Future System Architecture Through the Lens of Computer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00288)
*Hai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出智能计算架构模型（ICAM），一个六层框架用于模型原生计算，通过双平面视图（概率执行平面与确定性控制平面）解决LLM是更像CPU还是操作系统的问题，并引入三条设计定律（语义局部性定律、上下文预算定律、Agent加速定律），为模型原生计算提供统一的理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从模型技术向系统技术过渡，但现有的缓存复用、上下文管理、Agent调度、权限控制等工程问题缺乏统一的理论模型；尽管已有LLM-as-OS、记忆管理、Agent框架等多条研究线索，但它们分别处理同一系统的不同层次，缺少统一的体系结构视角。

Method: 采用愿景性综述方法，将计算机体系结构概念映射到模型原生堆栈；提出ICAM六层框架（含显式接口契约与设计公理），引入双平面视图和三定律，并基于已发表的系统级数据验证定律的有效性。

Result: 提出了ICAM六层框架、双平面视图（概率执行平面与确定性控制平面），以及三条设计定律：语义局部性定律（KV缓存复用与推理加速）、上下文预算定律（有限窗口与注意力衰减下的有效工作集）、Agent加速定律（多Agent协作的边际收益递减）。验证了定律与已有系统数据的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文是概念性与综述性贡献，不报告新实验；指出了类比失效的边界，并提出了模型原生计算的研究路线图。

Abstract: Large language models are undergoing a transition from model technology to system technology. As developers use Codex, Claude Code, AutoGPT, and related agents to write code, manage projects, and execute multi-step tasks, recurring engineering problems such as cache reuse, context management, agent scheduling, and permission control increasingly resemble classical computer systems problems. This paper develops that analogy as a visionary survey. We map concepts from computer architecture to the emerging model-native stack and review work on LLM-as-OS, memory management, agent frameworks, tool protocols, multi-agent coordination, cognitive architectures, and safety governance. We argue that these strands address different layers of the same system but lack a unified model.
  To fill this gap, we propose the Intelligent Computing Architecture Model (ICAM), a six-layer framework for model-native computing with explicit interface contracts and design axioms. ICAM resolves the apparent tension over whether an LLM is more like a CPU or an operating system through a dual-plane view: a probabilistic execution plane concerned with what can be computed, and a deterministic control plane concerned with what should be computed. We further introduce three design laws: the Semantic Locality Law for KV-cache reuse and inference speedup, the Context Budget Law for effective working sets under finite windows and attention decay, and the Agent Speedup Law for diminishing returns in multi-agent collaboration. We validate these laws against published system-level data and relate them to recent evidence on agentic software practices. We conclude by identifying where the analogy breaks down and outlining a research roadmap for model-native computing. This is a conceptual and survey contribution; it does not report new experiments.

</details>

*Edward W. Staley,Tom Arbaugh,Michael Pekala,Alexander New,Christopher D. Stiles,Nam Q. Le,Gregory Bassen,Wyatt Bunstine,Tyrel McQueen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合框架，结合热力学数据库和简化动力学模型来评估LLM在无机合成规划中的表现，以铌-氧系统为案例，发现LLM的隐式先验能产生比经典路径规划算法更可行的合成策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式ML模型可以提出具有目标性质的新型无机晶体材料，但这些材料的合成规划仍然困难，原因是相关物理过程复杂且计算工具有限。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的混合框架，将热力学数据库与简化动力学模型相结合，以近似逼真的合成条件，用于评估LLM在无机合成规划中的表现。以铌-氧系统为案例，在计算模拟中比较了LLM生成的合成路线与经典路径规划算法。

Result: 在计算模拟中，LLM生成的合成路线比经典路径规划算法能产生更可行的策略。经典搜索方法主要作为对照而非直接竞争对手。

Conclusion: LLM的隐式先验在无机合成规划中具有价值，该框架揭示了问题的相对复杂性，并突出了LLM隐式先验的增值之处。

Abstract: Modern generative machine learning (ML) models can propose novel inorganic crystalline materials with targeted properties; however, synthesis planning of these materials remains difficult due to the complexity of the associated physical processes and limited availability of computational tools. We introduce a novel hybrid framework to evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) in inorganic synthesis planning by combining thermodynamic databases with simplified kinetics models to approximate realistic synthesis conditions. As a case study, we focus on the niobium-oxygen system, which features multiple industrially relevant oxide phases with well-characterized data. In computational simulations, we compare LLM-generated synthesis routes with classical path-planning algorithms, showing that the implicit priors in LLMs can yield more viable strategies. In our evaluation setting, classical search methods serve primarily as a foil rather than a direct competitor. This illustrates the relative complexity of the problem and highlights where the LLM's implicit priors add value.

</details>


### [36] [Doing What They Say, Not What They Reason: Locating the Faithfulness Gap in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00476)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM代理在推理过程中是否真正遵循其陈述的推理（过程忠实性），通过分解为“推理-结论”和“结论-行动”两个步骤，在德州扑克模拟器中验证发现两者表现相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于社会模拟时，缺乏判断其行为是否忠实于所陈述推理的度量标准，且不存在正确的参考行为，因此需要研究过程忠实性。

Method: 在受控的德州扑克模拟器环境中，将忠实性差距分解为推理-结论和结论-行动两个步骤，每个决策都有可验证的参考动作。

Result: 两个步骤的行为表现相反。

Conclusion: LLM代理的推理与行动之间存在非平凡的忠实性问题，且不同步骤的忠实性表现不一致。

Abstract: Do LLM agents act on the reasoning they state? This question of process fidelity is central to using LLMs in social simulation, yet it is hard to measure where no reference for correct behavior exists. We study it in acontrolled setting, a Texas Poker simulator with a verifiable reference action for every decision by decomposing the faithfulness gap into two steps: reasoning-conclusion and conclusion-action. The two steps behave oppositely.

</details>


### [37] [Threshold-Based Exclusive Batching for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00516)
*Weifang Zhang,Yuzhou Nie,Bowen Pang,Guangrui Ma,Shining Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现混合批处理（MB）中prefill与decode之间存在干扰，导致边际成本高于纯解码。基于GPU显存带宽、模型大小和工作负载组合，推导出EB与MB性能交叉的闭式条件及最优切换阈值。优化后的EB在带宽受限GPU上吞吐量提升高达41.9%，提出的混合调度器EB+在非平稳流量下自动切换，性能优于MB最高达36.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合批处理（MB）虽已成为LLM推理的标准调度策略，但其prefill-decode干扰会导致边际成本增加。现有研究缺乏对该干扰的量化分析及在不同带宽GPU上的影响差异，因此需要系统研究何时应该选择EB而非MB，并设计自适应调度策略。

Method: 通过控制实验发现prefill-decode干扰导致MB边际成本上升；推导EB-MB性能交叉的闭式条件及最优相切换阈值；对高带宽GPU（H200）和带宽受限GPU（RTX PRO 6000）进行对比实验；设计混合调度器EB+，在线动态选择EB或MB。

Result: H200上decode token超过80%的batch时MB才出现干扰，而RTX PRO 6000上阈值仅为20%；优化EB在带宽受限GPU上吞吐量提升高达41.9%；EB+在非平稳流量下吞吐量最优或接近最优，相较MB最高提升36.4%。

Conclusion: MB与EB的最优选择取决于GPU显存带宽、模型大小和工作负载组合；高带宽硬件+大模型场景MB保持优势，带宽受限场景优化EB显著更优；EB+混合调度器可无人工干预地动态适应当前最优调度策略。

Abstract: Mixed batching (MB)--interleaving prefill and decode in a single batch--has become the standard scheduling strategy for large language model (LLM) inference due to its efficiency in maximizing compute and memory utilization. However, through controlled experiments, we find that prefill-decode interference inflates MB's per-step marginal cost above that of pure decode. On the high-bandwidth H200 (4.8 TB/s), this occurs only when decode tokens exceed 80% of the batch; however, on the bandwidth-constrained RTX PRO 6000 (1.792 TB/s), this threshold plummets to just 20%. Consequently, the optimal choice between MB and exclusive batching (EB) fundamentally depends on GPU memory bandwidth, model size, and workload composition. We derive a closed-form condition for this EB-MB performance crossover, along with asymptotically optimal phase-switching thresholds and memory-safe batch sizing for EB. Optimized EB achieves up to 41.9% higher throughput on bandwidth-constrained GPUs, while MB retains its advantage on high-bandwidth hardware with larger models. Our hybrid scheduler EB+ applies this condition online to dynamically switch between EB and MB without manual intervention. Under non-stationary traffic with distribution or concurrency shifts, EB+ attains the highest or near-highest throughput in every setting, outperforming MB by up to 36.4%.

</details>


### [38] [Probe Before You Edit: Probing-Guided Molecular Optimization for LLM Agents in Structure-Based Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00555)
*Zaifei Yang,Weiyu Chen,Yaqing Wang,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PROBE框架通过编辑-响应探测(edit-response probing)解决药物设计中结合亲和力与成药性难以同时优化的问题，在CrossDocked2020基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在结构药物设计中单步编辑难以同时改善结合亲和力和成药性这两个往往冲突的目标，缺乏对口袋-配体复合物对局部修改如何响应的理解，导致联合改进极少实现。

Method: 提出PROBE框架：(1) 将配体分解为可编辑位点，构建口袋特异的site map标记联合增益可行区、目标冲突区和应改责任子结构；(2) 执行受控探测编辑，将响应提炼为EditManual；(3) 运行多智能体迭代循环（亲和力智能体、成药性智能体、协同优化智能体）联合生成编辑。

Result: 在CrossDocked2020基准测试上达到SOTA性能，显著缓解了诊断指标暴露的失效模式。

Conclusion: 通过模拟药物化学家对口袋-配体复合物进行受控类似物编辑后再选择优化方向的策略，PROBE能有效提升联合优化成功率，为基于LLM的结构药物设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design increasingly employs LLM agents to iteratively refine ligands against a target pocket, yet a viable ligand must satisfy two often-conflicting objectives -- binding affinity and druggability -- which single optimization steps rarely improve together. To quantify this difficulty, we introduce two diagnostic metrics: the first measures how often a single edit improves both objectives, and the second measures how often a gain on one objective comes with a loss on the other. Applying these diagnostics to current LLM-agent pipelines exposes a consistent failure mode: the agent performs molecular editing without knowing how the pocket-ligand complex responds to local modifications, thus rarely achieving joint improvement. Inspired by medicinal chemists, who probe the pocket-ligand complex with controlled analog edits before choosing an optimization direction, we propose \textbf{PROBE}, an optimization framework built around edit-response probing. PROBE first decomposes the ligand into editable sites and builds a pocket-specific \textbf{site map} that flags where joint gains are plausible, where the two objectives are likely in tension, and where liability substructures should be changed; it then performs controlled probe edits whose responses are distilled into an \textbf{EditManual}. Guided by the site map and EditManual, PROBE runs an iterative multi-agent loop in which an affinity agent, a druggability agent, and a co-optimization agent jointly produce edits. On the CrossDocked2020 benchmark, PROBE achieves state-of-the-art performance and substantially mitigates the failure modes exposed by our diagnostics metrics.

</details>


### [39] [PropLLM: Propagation-Aware Scene Reconstruction for Network Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00582)
*Zongzong Wu,Ming Zhao,Fengxiao Tang,Nei Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PropLLM首次将逐跳场景重建范式与大语言模型的生成推理能力结合，通过双层次知识图谱和时序因果传播注意力机制，沿传播路径反向追踪定位网络故障根因，在Wi-Fi多模态故障数据集上故障类型诊断准确率提升3.9%，根因定位准确率提升4.7%，幻觉率降低50.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络故障诊断方法（基于规则、机器学习或大语言模型）均以单次映射方式将告警集映射到诊断结果，无法解决端点模糊性问题——不同根因故障可能产生高度相似的最终症状。

Method: 提出PropLLM，采用逐跳场景重建范式，从端点告警出发沿传播路径反向追踪；每跳从双层次知识图谱检索可验证事实证据；提出时序因果传播注意力（TCPA）机制，将已知拓扑因果先验直接编码到注意力计算中，引导模型沿正确因果方向推理，最终通过完整因果链定位根因并确定故障类型。

Result: 在真实Wi-Fi多模态故障数据集上，故障类型诊断准确率提升3.9%，根因定位准确率提升4.7%，幻觉率降低50.8%；在TeleLogs 5G数据集上的补充实验也验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PropLLM通过逐跳推理与知识图谱增强的事实证据检索，有效解决了网络故障诊断中的端点模糊性问题，显著降低了LLM的幻觉率，并适用于不同网络场景。

Abstract: Network faults propagate layer by layer along topology and protocol dependencies, yet operations systems typically observe only symptomatic alerts at the tail end of propagation chains, where distinct root-cause faults may produce highly similar end-point symptoms. Existing approaches, whether rule-based, machine learning (ML)-based, or large language model (LLM)-based, fundamentally map the alert set to a diagnosis in a single pass and are structurally incapable of resolving this end-point ambiguity. This paper proposes PropLLM, which is the first to integrate the hop-by-hop scene reconstruction paradigm with the generative reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Starting from end-point alerts, PropLLM traces back hop-by-hop along the propagation path, retrieving verifiable factual evidence from a dual-layer knowledge graph (KG) at each hop, while the proposed Temporal Causal Propagation Attention (TCPA) mechanism encodes known topological causal priors directly into the attention computation to guide the model along the correct causal direction, ultimately localizing the root cause and determining the fault type through a fully evidenced causal chain. On a real-world Wi-Fi multimodal fault dataset, PropLLM improves fault type diagnosis accuracy by 3.9\% and root cause localization accuracy by 4.7\% over the strongest baseline, while reducing the hallucination rate by 50.8\%. Supplementary experiments on the TeleLogs 5G dataset further demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method across different network scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [TRACE: Trajectory Risk-Aware Compression for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00611)
*Zhepei Hong,Lin Wang,Liting Li,Haokai Ma,Junfeng Fang,Fei Shen,Dan Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE提出了一种轨迹级风险感知压缩方法，通过Compressor-Reader架构将长程智能体轨迹压缩为潜在证据状态，用于安全检测，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体产生的安全证据分布在长轨迹中，存在稀疏、延迟和组合性风险信号，而现有的turn级或短上下文检测器难以在扩展时间范围内可靠地保留和聚合这些证据。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，采用Compressor-Reader设计：Compressor在轨迹级监督下将完整轨迹编码为紧凑的潜在证据状态，Reader以该潜在证据状态作为安全参考来判断原始轨迹。

Result: 在ASSEBench、Pre-Ex-Bench和R-Judge上，TRACE在所有评估骨干网络上取得最佳准确率，比强基线提升高达12.6个百分点。在LongSafety上，随着上下文长度增长，TRACE的性能退化更小。

Conclusion: 压缩的参考证据帮助Reader聚焦于风险关键片段并恢复跨步骤证据，验证了轨迹级证据压缩对长程智能体安全检测的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents produce safety evidence across long trajectories, where sparse, delayed, and compositional risk signals often escape local moderation. Existing turn-level or short-context detectors struggle to reliably retain and aggregate such evidence over extended horizons. We reframe long-horizon agent safety detection as trajectory-level evidence compression and propose Trajectory Risk-Aware Compression for Long-Horizon Agent Safety (TRACE). TRACE uses a Compressor-Reader design: the Compressor encodes the full trajectory into a compact latent evidence state under trajectory-level supervision, and the Reader judges the raw trajectory with this latent evidence state as a safety reference. This design helps aggregate dispersed risk cues and reduce premature evidence loss. Across ASSEBench, Pre-Ex-Bench, and R-Judge, TRACE achieves the best accuracy on all evaluated backbones, improving over strong baselines by up to 12.6 percentage points. On LongSafety, TRACE shows smaller performance degradation as context length grows. Attention visualizations and case studies suggest that the compressed reference helps the Reader focus on risk-critical segments and recover cross-step evidence. Code is available at https://github.com/Peregrine123/TRACE_official.

</details>


### [41] [ForeSci: Evaluating LLM Agents for Forward-Looking AI Research Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00644)
*Qiuyu Tian,Zequn Liu,Yingce Xia,Haojie Yin,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeSci，一个时间受控的基准测试，用于评估LLM代理能否基于历史证据做出前瞻性研究判断。该基准包含500个任务，涵盖四个快速发展的AI领域和四类决策家族。每个任务配有时间对齐的离线知识库，截止日期后的论文被隐藏仅用于验证。实验评估了原生LLM、混合RAG和三种研究代理适配方法。结果表明显式证据组织提高了可追溯性和事实支持，但收益强烈依赖于决策类型。诊断发现存在证据-决策解耦问题：代理可能引用相关证据却预测错误的研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究常需在缺乏未来证据的情况下做出决策（如选择瓶颈、方向或项目定位），但现有基准缺乏对前瞻性研究判断能力的系统性评估。ForeSci旨在弥补这一空白，将前瞻性AI研究判断转化为受控的基准测试。

Method: 构建500个任务，覆盖四个快速发展的AI领域和四类决策家族。每个任务配对截止时间对齐的离线知识库，截止日期后的论文被隐藏仅用于验证。任务源自截止日期前的分类分支和证据信号，答案生成骨干模型的选择早于任务截止日期。评估了原生LLM、混合RAG和三种研究代理适配方法，使用四种骨干模型。

Result: 显式证据组织提高了可追溯性和事实支持，但收益强烈依赖于决策类型。诊断发现存在证据-决策解耦问题：代理可能引用相关证据却预测错误的研究对象。

Conclusion: ForeSci将前瞻性AI研究判断转化为受控基准，可用于评估研究代理作为决策系统的能力。现有方法在证据组织上有所改进，但证据与决策之间仍存在解耦问题，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: AI research often requires decisions before future evidence exists: which bottleneck to attack, which direction to pursue, or where a project should be positioned. We introduce ForeSci, a temporally controlled benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can make such forward-looking research judgements from historical evidence. ForeSci contains 500 tasks across four fast-moving AI domains and four decision families. Each task is paired with a cutoff-aligned offline knowledge base; post-cutoff papers are hidden during generation and used only for validation. To avoid random future-event prediction, tasks are derived from pre-cutoff taxonomy branches and evidence signals, and answer-generation backbones are selected to precede the task cutoffs. We evaluate native LLMs, Hybrid RAG, and three research-agent adaptations across four backbones. Results show that explicit evidence organization improves traceability and factual support, but gains depend strongly on the decision family. Diagnostics reveal a recurring evidence-decision decoupling: agents may cite relevant evidence while forecasting the wrong research object. ForeSci turns forward-looking AI research judgement into a controlled benchmark for evaluating research agents as decision-making systems.

</details>


### [42] [MOSAIC: Modular Orchestration for Structured Agentic Intelligence and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00708)
*Yifan Bao,Xinyu Xi,Xinyu Liu,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Kevin Zhang,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC框架，将自动化数据科学建模为结构化、记忆驱动的模型选择问题，通过语义任务画像、蓝图构建、执行验证和失败感知强化学习，在金融时序预测与生成任务上优于AutoML和智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML系统在预定义流水线空间内搜索，灵活性不足；LLM智能体虽更灵活但建模决策非结构化、难以验证和复用。需要一种既能利用LLM灵活性又保持结构化、可追溯、可复用的模型选择方法。

Method: MOSAIC框架包含：1) 语义任务画像构建；2) 基于记忆的先前案例与代码模块检索；3) 蓝图(blueprint)作为中间表示，指定建模组件、组合方式、接口约束和执行需求；4) 基于检索证据的LLM代码生成；5) 执行验证与诊断反馈；6) 失败感知强化学习策略用于细化。

Result: 在金融时间序列预测与生成任务上，MOSAIC在任务性能、执行成功率和决策可追溯性方面均优于AutoML和智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: 将自动化数据科学视为结构化的、可复用的、执行驱动的模型选择问题是有价值的，MOSAIC框架验证了这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Automated data science is a structured model-selection problem. A solution must choose data transformations, feature representations, architecture, training procedure, evaluation protocol, and refinement strategy for a task. AutoML systems automate parts of this process, but typically search within predefined pipeline, model, and hyperparameter spaces. LLM-based agents offer greater flexibility through retrieval, code generation, and execution feedback, yet their modelling decisions are often unstructured, difficult to verify, and hard to reuse. We introduce \textsc{MOSAIC} (Modular Orchestration for Structured Agentic Intelligence and Composition), a structured agentic framework for memory-grounded model selection and workflow construction. Given a task and dataset, \textsc{MOSAIC} builds a semantic task profile, retrieves prior cases and source-code modules, and constructs a blueprint: an intermediate representation specifying selected modelling components, composition, interface constraints, and execution requirements. This blueprint turns model selection into a staged, context-grounded search and grounds LLM-based code generation in retrieved evidence rather than unconstrained synthesis. Candidate models are validated by execution and refined using diagnostic feedback, training traces, task metrics, and a failure-aware reinforcement learning policy. We instantiate \textsc{MOSAIC} on financial time-series forecasting and generation, where models must satisfy predictive accuracy, distributional fidelity, execution reliability, and downstream financial criteria such as risk and tail behaviour. Experiments against AutoML and agentic baselines show that \textsc{MOSAIC} improves task performance, execution success, and decision traceability, demonstrating the value of treating automated data science as structured, reusable, and execution-grounded model selection.

</details>


### [43] [LLM-Driven Co-Evolutionary Automated Heuristic Design for Bi-Component Coupled Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00718)
*Mingen Kuang,Xudong Deng,Xi Lin,Ye Fan,Jianyong Sun,Jialong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoEvo-AHD框架，利用LLM驱动双种群协同进化来自动设计耦合组合优化问题的启发式算法，在TTP和TPP上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动启发式设计方法仅将启发式作为单一算子或搜索策略进行独立进化，无法建模多决策子结构间的强耦合关系（如TTP、TPP问题）。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的双种群协同进化框架，包含：1) 双种群协同进化路线与选择算子；2) 合作评估机制显式捕获算子间交互；3) 成对评分与协同联合交叉发现互补算子逻辑；4) 工具调用环境库封装核心操作（如局部搜索delta计算）为标准接口。

Result: 在TTP和TPP上的实验表明，CoEvo-AHD自动发现协同启发式组合，取得优于传统启发式的解质量。

Conclusion: 通过LLM驱动的双种群协同进化，可有效建模耦合决策子空间中的算子交互，自动发现互补启发式组合，提升耦合组合优化问题的求解质量。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promise in Automated Heuristic Design (AHD), existing methods typically generate and evolve heuristics as a single operator or search strategy, limiting their ability to model strong coupling among multiple decision substructures in problems such as the Traveling Thief Problem (TTP) and the Traveling Purchaser Problem (TPP). In this work, we propose CoEvo-AHD, an LLM-driven dual-population co-evolutionary framework for automated heuristic design in coupled combinatorial optimization. Unlike prior methods that evolve individual heuristics in isolation, CoEvo-AHD leverages LLMs to co-evolve two closely related operator populations. A cooperative evaluation mechanism explicitly captures interactions between route and selection operators, while pairwise scoring and synergistic joint crossover help discover complementary operator logic for joint improvement across coupled decision subspaces. We further design a tool-invocation environment library that encapsulates frequently used core operations, such as local-search delta computation, into callable functions, enabling LLM-generated operators to use standardized interfaces instead of reimplementing inefficient and error-prone problem-specific loops. Experiments on TTP and TPP show that CoEvo-AHD automatically discovers cooperative heuristic combinations and achieves competitive solution quality against traditional heuristics.

</details>


### [44] [Latent Reward Steering: An Adaptive Inference-Time Framework that Implicitly Promotes Cognitive Behaviors in Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00726)
*Jiakang Li,Guanyu Zhu,Can Jin,Chenxi Huang,Dexu Yu,Ronghao Chen,Yang Zhou,Hongwu Peng,Xuanqi Lan,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Youhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Latent Reward Steering (LRS)，一种自适应推理时框架，通过优化隐含认知行为的稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜在状态来提升推理能力，避免显式行为控制。训练潜在奖励模型评估中间状态质量，利用奖励梯度提供状态特定的纠正方向，并通过奖励与置信度门控限制干预。实验表明LRS持续提升性能，并能隐式促进良好认知行为以修复推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理方法依赖显式行为级控制，在面对不同推理状态、任务和模型时适应性不足，无法有效处理多样化的失败模式和所需的纠正行为。

Method: 1) 训练潜在奖励模型（Latent Reward Model），基于推理轨迹的最终答案正确性评估中间SAE潜在状态的质量；2) 推理时，对奖励信号标记为脆弱的潜在状态，利用奖励梯度提供状态特定的纠正方向；3) 通过奖励与置信度门控（reward and confidence gate）限制干预范围，仅作用于需要修正的状态。

Result: 在多个推理LLM骨干网络和基准测试上，LRS持续优于各类基线方法。事后分析表明LRS隐式促进了良好的认知行为，能够修复原始推理错误。

Conclusion: 通过隐式优化SAE潜在状态中的认知行为，LRS提供了一种自适应的推理时干预框架，相比显式行为控制方法更灵活有效，能够推广到不同模型和任务。

Abstract: Strong reasoning depends not only on model knowledge but also on how effectively cognitive behaviors are deployed during generation. Existing methods often rely on explicit behavior-level control, making them insufficiently adaptive when failures and required corrections vary across reasoning states, tasks, and models. To this end, we propose Latent Reward Steering (LRS), an adaptive inference-time framework that promotes cognitive behaviors by optimizing the sparse-autoencoder (SAE) latent states that implicitly carry them. Rather than relying on predefined cognitive behaviors or steering directions derived from them, LRS trains a latent reward model on reasoning traces by final answer correctness to estimate the quality of intermediate latent states. During inference, reward gradients provide state-specific correction directions for fragile latent states, while a reward and confidence gate restricts intervention to states the reward signal flags as fragile. Experiments on multiple reasoning LLM backbones and benchmarks show that \ours consistently improves performance over various baselines, and post-hoc analyses further indicate that \ours implicitly promotes good cognitive behaviors that fix the original reasoning errors. Code is available at: https://github.com/jiakanglee/Latent-Reward-Steering.

</details>


### [45] [Property Prediction of Stacked Bilayer Materials: A Multimodal Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01012)
*An Vuong,Minh-Hao Van,Chen Zhao,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态学习方法，用于研究双层二维材料堆叠中的异质界面，预测垂直集成下产生的新性质，实验证明该方法优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管双层二维材料在实验和计算上已取得显著进展，但利用AI建模双层堆叠并预测新性质的研究仍不充分，亟需进一步探索。

Method: 提出一种新颖的多模态学习方法，研究不同功能材料层在给定配置下垂直堆叠形成的异质界面，联合预测新产生的性质。

Result: 综合实验表明，该方法相比基线方法在有效性和效率上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 多模态学习能够有效建模双层二维材料堆叠中的异质界面，为加速新材料发现提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI for materials science is a critical topic within AI for science, aiming to accelerate materials discovery and produce accurate property predictions. Bilayer 2D material stacking is essential for exploring new materials with novel functions and inherent phenomena, enabling the creation of new 2D bilayers for diverse real-world applications. Research on bilayer vdWs materials has made significant progress from experimental and computational perspectives. Various bilayer materials have been successfully synthe sized experimentally and the increasing utilization of high-throughput computing technology has con structed several computational two-dimensional materials databases. However, the use of AI to model bilayer stacking and predict new properties remains underexplored, necessitating further research studies. In this work, we propose a novel multimodal learning approach to study the interfaces between dissimilar materials that jointly enable new or multiple functions, and to predict new properties arising from the vertical integration (stacking) of different functional material layers under given configurations. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our approach compared to baseline methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/AnVuong123/bimat ml.

</details>


### [46] [CoMIC: Collaborative Memory and Insights Circulation for Long-Horizon LLM Agents in Cloud-Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00756)
*Yannan Wang,Longli Yang,Zhen Liu,Abhishek Kumar,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoMIC是一个无需参数更新的云-边协作框架，通过中心化反思与去中心化执行的设计，让轻量级边缘LLM代理借助云端批评者的异步评估和经验聚合，在长时域任务中提升性能，无需更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘服务器上部署轻量级LLM代理时，这些模型难以处理需要持久记忆、子目标追踪和反思的长时域任务；对边缘模型进行后部署微调成本高且难以跨异构节点扩展，而纯本地记忆会导致代理经验孤立且提示上下文不断增长。

Method: 提出CoMIC框架，采用"中心化反思、去中心化执行"设计：边缘代理使用面向子目标的分层记忆和选择性重新展开相关历史进行本地执行；云端LLM批评者异步评估完成的轨迹、过滤可复用经验、并按语义子目标标识符聚合跨代理指导。

Result: 在涵盖符号规划和文本交互的五项长时域代理任务上，CoMIC提升了弱边缘代理的进度率和动作接地性，在不更新模型参数的情况下取得了依赖任务的成功率提升。

Conclusion: CoMIC证明了无需参数更新的云-边协作方式能够有效增强轻量级边缘代理在长时域任务中的表现，为边缘AI服务的可扩展部署提供了一种低成本方案。

Abstract: Deploying lightweight Large Language Model (LLM) agents on edge servers can reduce latency and move agentic services closer to users, but resource-constrained edge models often struggle with long-horizon tasks that require persistent memory, subgoal tracking, and reflection. Fine-tuning edge models after deployment is costly and difficult to scale across heterogeneous nodes, while purely local memory leaves agents with isolated experience and growing prompt context. We propose \textsc{CoMIC}, a parameter-update-free cloud-edge framework for Collaborative Memory and Insights Circulation. \textsc{CoMIC} follows a \textit{Centralized Reflection, Decentralized Execution} design: edge agents execute locally using subgoal-oriented hierarchical memory and selective re-expansion of relevant histories, while a cloud-side LLM critic asynchronously evaluates completed trajectories, filters reusable experience, and aggregates cross-agent guidance keyed by semantic subgoal identifiers. Across five long-horizon agent tasks spanning symbolic planning and text interaction, \textsc{CoMIC} improves progress rate and action grounding for weak edge agents and yields task-dependent success-rate gains without updating model parameters.

</details>


### [47] [FALAT: Tracing Failures in LLM Agent Trajectories via Dependency-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00765)
*Md Nakhla Rafi,Md Ahasanuzzaman,Dong Jae Kim,Zhijie Wang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FALAT框架，通过依赖引导的搜索对LLM代理轨迹中的故障进行归因，在Who&When基准上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在执行复杂长轨迹任务时经常失败，但现有方法无法有效区分错误引入步骤和错误传播步骤，故障归因困难。

Method: FALAT将故障归因建模为依赖引导的搜索问题：先构建任务预期解，识别轨迹中的可疑区域；然后追溯决策、工具输出和代理消息间的依赖关系，区分错误引入与错误传播；最后评估纠正候选步骤是否足以恢复预期结果，从而定位责任代理和决定性失败步骤。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，算法生成轨迹的步骤级准确率达46.0%，手工构建轨迹达29.1%，优于专用归因基线和直接提示独立LLM的方法。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的推理对于LLM代理系统的可靠故障诊断至关重要，FALAT证明了结构化依赖追踪在故障归因中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly solve complex tasks through long trajectories involving reasoning steps, tool calls, and inter-agent communication. However, when these agents fail, it is often unclear which agent caused the failure and which step introduced the decisive error. This attribution problem is challenging because mistakes can propagate across the trajectory: later actions may appear incorrect, but only because they depend on an earlier corrupted state. Therefore, failure attribution cannot be treated as independent step-level classification.
  We propose FALAT, a diagnostic framework for failure attribution in LLM agent trajectories. FALAT frames attribution as a dependency-guided search problem. It first constructs an expectation of how the task should be solved and uses this expectation to identify suspicious regions in the trajectory. It then traces dependencies among decisions, tool outputs, and agent messages to distinguish error-introducing steps from steps that merely inherit or propagate prior mistakes. Finally, FALAT evaluates whether correcting a candidate step would be sufficient to recover the expected outcome, allowing it to identify both the responsible agent and the decisive failure step.
  We evaluate FALAT on the Who&When benchmark, which includes both algorithm-generated and hand-crafted multi-agent failure trajectories. The results show that FALAT consistently improves responsible-agent and decisive-step attribution. Its best configurations achieve 46.0% step-level accuracy on algorithm-generated trajectories and 29.1% on the more challenging hand-crafted trajectories, outperforming specialized attribution baselines and direct prompting with standalone LLMs. These findings suggest that dependency-aware reasoning is essential for reliable failure diagnosis in LLM agent systems.

</details>


### [48] [Ryze: Evidence-Enriched Data Synthesis from Biomedical Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00902)
*Yeqi Huang,Yue Chen,Yanwei Ye,Guanhao Su,Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ryze是一个全自动系统，能从原始生物医学论文中生成富含证据的训练数据，并基于Qwen3-VL-8B以不到200美元的成本训练出BioVLM-8B模型，在LAB-Bench上达到48.0%加权准确率，超越基础模型12.6个百分点和GPT-5.2的3.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLMs在生物医学研究中不可靠，因为科学论文中的正确答案依赖于分散在图、表、图表、标题和引用文本中的证据；现有后训练流程受限于昂贵的专家标注和会丢失证据结构的合成数据。

Method: Ryze包含三个核心组件：(1) 从原始生物医学论文中合成带有完整支持证据（视觉元素、标题、提取结构、引用段落）的QA对；(2) 通过图表/表格感知提取和LLM清洗减少布局和OCR错误；(3) 采用渐进门控后训练策略，结合监督微调和强化学习。

Result: BioVLM-8B在LAB-Bench上达到48.0%加权准确率，比Qwen3-VL-8B基础模型提升12.6个百分点，并超越GPT-5.2达3.8个百分点，总训练成本低于200美元。

Conclusion: Ryze证明了通过全自动、低成本的证据增强训练流程可以显著提升生物医学领域的VLM性能，并开源了系统及训练好的BioVLM-8B模型。

Abstract: General-purpose VLMs remain unreliable for biomedical research because valid answers in scientific papers depend on evidence split across figures, tables, charts, captions, and referring text. Existing post-training pipelines are bottlenecked by costly expert annotation and by synthetic data that drops this evidence structure. We present Ryze, a fully automated system that converts raw biomedical papers into an evidence-enriched training set and a domain-specialized VLM. Ryze synthesizes QA pairs with complete supporting evidence (visual element, caption, extracted structure, and referring paragraphs), reduces layout and OCR errors via chart/table-aware extraction and LLM-based cleansing, and applies a progress-gated post-training strategy combining supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Starting from Qwen3-VL-8B, Ryze produces BioVLM-8B at under USD 200, achieving 48.0% weighted accuracy on LAB-Bench, outperforming the base model by +12.6 percentage points (pp) and surpassing GPT-5.2 by +3.8 pp. We release Ryze as open source together with the trained BioVLM-8B model.

</details>


### [49] [Adversarial Feeds Steer LLM Agent Decisions Against Their Defaults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00914)
*Rana Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控实验协议，通过固定模型、角色、主题和决策提示，仅改变智能体在"滚动"阶段看到的帖子内容和顺序，证明了信息流排序（推荐系统）对LLM智能体下游决策存在因果影响，且该影响具有剂量-反应曲线、默认方向不对称性等特征，呼吁智能体评估必须审计信息流层而非仅关注最终提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估仅测试模型或用户提示本身，忽略了上游排名器（推荐系统）对智能体行为的影响，而智能体在实际部署中几乎总是在消费经过排序的外部信息流后采取行动。

Method: 设计受控实验协议：固定模型、角色、主题和最终决策提示，仅在十轮"滚动"阶段改变帖子组成和排序，隔离信息流策划对下游决策的因果效应。在4个开源指令LLM上进行了2,785次决策回滚实验。

Result: 识别出三种响应模式：对抗性屈服、默认饱和、默认方向不对称（单侧信息流可将模型真正不确定的决策从5%翻转至100%，Fisher p值低至3×10⁻¹⁰，但无法动摇已偏好的决策）。效应遵循剂量-反应曲线，通过生成器替换排除了写作风格伪影，在多个决策领域（包括安全相关决策如移除部署审批关卡、放宽访问控制）表现出泛化性，两种简单的信息流级防御可部分缓解，前沿模型保留其默认行为。

Conclusion: 将推荐系统定性为LLM智能体的实用、默认有界的控制面，主张智能体评估必须审计信息流层而非仅审计最终提示。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act after consuming ranked external information streams such as social feeds, search results, retrieval contexts, and email queues, yet safety evaluations almost always test the model or the user prompt in isolation, never the upstream ranker that decides what the agent reads just before it acts. We introduce a controlled protocol that holds the model, persona, topic, and final decision prompt fixed and varies only the composition and ordering of the posts an agent encounters during a preceding ten-turn "scrolling" phase, isolating the causal effect of feed curation on a downstream decision. Across 2,785 decision rollouts on four modern open instruct LLMs from three independent labs, we identify three response regimes: adversarial capitulation, default saturation, and a default-direction asymmetry in which a one-sided feed tips a decision the model was genuinely uncertain about (in the clearest cases from 5% to 100%; Fisher p as low as 3 x 10^-10) but cannot dislodge one it already favors or holds firmly. The effect follows a dose-response curve, survives a generator swap that rules out a writing-style artifact, generalizes across several decision domains including security-relevant choices such as removing a deployment approval gate or relaxing access controls, and is partly mitigated by two simple feed-level defenses; a frontier model retains its default. We characterize the recommender as a practical, default-bounded control surface for LLM agents, and argue that agent evaluations must audit the feed layer rather than the final prompt alone.

</details>


### [50] [Large Language Models in Transportation Systems Management and Operations: From Text Reasoning to Multi-modal Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00991)
*Siyan Li,Zehao Wang,Jiachen Li,Kanok Boriboonsomsin,Matthew J. Barth,Guoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLMs）和多模态大语言模型（MM-LLMs）在交通系统管理与运营（TSMO）中的应用，涵盖运营与服务、出行与车队服务、数据建模与决策支持三大领域，通过PRISMA流程筛选文献，识别了数据异构性、实时推理、可解释性、多模态融合和治理等挑战，并指出了本地化适配、边缘部署、基准测试和跨机构合作等未来方向。LLMs最适用于决策支持层，MM-LLMs在异构文本、视觉和传感器数据整合方面具有特殊价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSMO日益依赖对异构数据（传感器流、事故报告、用户反馈、视觉观测等）的实时解读，传统方法难以有效整合这些结构化与非结构化信息。LLMs和MM-LLMs的出现为将这些多样化输入集成到面向操作员的决策支持系统提供了新的机制。

Method: 采用PRISMA指导的筛选流程对现有研究进行系统综述，将文献按三个领域分类：运输运营与服务（供给侧）、出行与车队服务（需求侧）、数据建模与决策支持，并区分了操作性应用与原型/新兴概念。

Result: 识别出数据异构性、实时推理、可解释性、多模态融合和治理等反复出现的挑战；区分了已投入运营的应用和仍处于原型阶段的概念；指出LLMs最有前景的角色是作为决策支持层，MM-LLMs在处理文本、视觉和传感器等多模态异构数据时具有独特优势。

Conclusion: LLM-based系统在TSMO中作为决策支持层最具潜力，MM-LLMs在整合异构文本、视觉和传感器输入方面价值突出。未来方向包括本地化适配、边缘部署、基准测试和跨机构协作。

Abstract: Transportation systems management and operations (TSMO) increasingly depends on timely interpretation of heterogeneous data, from various sensor streams, incident reports, traveler feedback, and visual observations. Large language models (LLMs), including emerging multi-modal large language models (MM-LLMs), provide a new mechanism for integrating these structured and unstructured inputs into operator-facing decision support. This survey paper reviews LLM- and MM-LLM-based applications in TSMO across three domains: transportation operations & services (supply), mobility & fleet services (demand), and data, modeling & decision support. Using a PRISMA-guided screening process, we synthesize current studies while distinguishing operationally oriented applications from prototype and emerging concepts. We further identify recurring challenges in data heterogeneity, real-time inference, explainability, multi-modal fusion, and governance. Finally, we outline existing gaps and future directions in localized adaptation, edge deployment, benchmarking, and cross-agency collaboration. Overall, LLM-based systems appear most promising as a decision-support layer, with MM-LLMs offering particular value when heterogeneous text, visual, and sensor inputs must be integrated.

</details>


### [51] [Can AI Review Improve Paper Drafting? An Empirical Study on 20 Computer Architecture Submissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01013)
*Di Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI审稿是否能够改进论文草稿撰写，通过构建AI-Paper-Review工具对20篇计算机架构论文进行案例研究，发现AI审稿能覆盖人类提出的多数问题，同时还能发现人类遗漏的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成论文数量激增给同行评审带来压力，导致AI生成审稿的使用，但相关伦理问题（保密性、质量、公平性）尚未达成共识。作者提出一个替代性实用问题：AI审稿能否改进论文草稿撰写？

Method: 研究20篇不同投稿层次的计算机架构论文，定义一组度量指标量化AI审稿与人类审稿的一致性；构建集成Web UI的工具AI-Paper-Review，从多样化的AI审稿人池中选择审稿人，基于评论的共性和重要性进行聚类和排序，并支持AI评论与人类评论的对齐以进行基于度量的验证。

Result: AI审稿能覆盖人类提出问题的显著部分，同时也能提出人类审稿中遗漏的问题。

Conclusion: 本文不鼓励在当前阶段使用AI进行同行评审，而是旨在研究AI审稿如何改进论文草稿撰写，以及AI同行评审的潜力与局限性。工具和案例数据的发布旨在推动该领域的未来研究。

Abstract: Research is advancing faster than ever with artificial intelligence (AI); and so are the corresponding research papers. The exploding volume of AI-generated papers have put a strain to peer review, leading to the usage of AI-generated review, potentially wide yet sneaky. However, relevant ethical concerns about confidentiality, quality, and fairness are raised and no consensus has been reached in the broad research community. We expect the debate to continue for a while, but in the meantime, we ask an alternative, practical question: \textit{can AI review improve paper drafting?}
  We study 20 computer architecture papers, with varying levels of submission lineage, to expose how well AI review aligns with human review, quantified by a set of metrics we define. To conduct the case study, we build a web UI-integrated tool, \emph{AI-Paper-Review}, that generates structured AI review of a draft paper, available at https://github.com/unarylab/ai-paper-review. This tool selects several AI reviewers from a diverse pool of AI reviewers and clusters and ranks their comments based on commonality and importance of review comments. It also allows to align AI comments with human comments to facilitate metric-based validation. The case study shows that AI review can cover a significant fraction of human-raised issues, but also raises issues missing in human review.
  This paper is not intended to encourage using AI for peer review at the current stage, but to study that (1) how AI review can improve paper drafting and (2) the potential and limitation of AI-based peer review. The release of the tool and the case study data is intended to instigate future research on this topic. Misuse for peer review would violate the ethics policies from major academic venues.

</details>


### [52] [TravelEval: A Comprehensive Benchmarking Framework for Evaluating LLM-Powered Travel Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01046)
*Weiyi Chen,Shuaixiong Wang,Ziyun Gao,Kaichun Hu,Wangze Ni,Shimin Di,Chen Jason Zhang,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TravelEval是一个面向LLM驱动的旅行规划评估基准，包含六维评估框架、高真实度数据沙箱和基于仿真的全局评估方法，揭示LLM在多维全局规划（尤其是时空推理和预算合规）方面存在困难，且智能体推理策略无一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划基准存在三个局限：1) 过度关注约束合规性，忽视时空成本等多维质量；2) 数据集缺乏真实性和关键领域覆盖（如住宿、交通）；3) 孤立评估每日计划，遗漏住宿影响和游览节奏等关键细节。TravelEval旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出六维评估框架（准确性、合规性、时间性、空间性、经济性、实用性）；2) 构建包含精准住宿定价和真实城际交通数据的高真实度数据沙箱；3) 设计基于仿真的全局评估方法，集成API地理信息和细粒度排队时间模拟完整旅行计划。

Result: 评估12种主流方法发现：LLMs在全局优化的多维规划中表现困难（尤其在时空推理和预算合规方面），且智能体推理策略并未带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: TravelEval通过基于真实时空仿真和综合指标为旅行计划评估提供了稳健基础，有助于推进LLM驱动的旅行规划研究与应用。

Abstract: The development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has significantly improved travel planning applications, yet evaluating such models is limited by existing benchmarks' limitations: 1) overemphasis on constraint compliance, neglecting multi-dimensional qualities like spatio-temporal cost; 2) datasets lacking real-world authenticity and coverage in key areas (e.g., lodging, transport); and 3) isolated daily plan assessments that miss critical details (e.g., the impact of daily accommodation and visit pacing) needed for entire plan's evaluation. To address this gap, we introduce TravelEval, a realistic and comprehensive benchmark. TravelEval features 1) a novel six-dimensional evaluation framework to holistically assess plans across accuracy, compliance, temporality, spatiality, economy, and utility dimensions; 2) a highly realistic data sandbox with precise accommodation pricing and authentic intercity transportation data; and 3) a simulation-based global evaluation method that emulates complete travel plans with API-integrated geographic information and fine-grained queuing time. Evaluating 12 mainstream approaches with TravelEval reveals several valuable insights, such that LLMs struggle with globally-optimized multi-dimensional planning (especially in spatio-temporal reasoning and budget compliance), and agentic reasoning strategies offer no consistent improvement. Concisely, TravelEval facilitates travel plan evaluation via grounded spatio-temporal emulation and comprehensive metrics, providing a robust foundation for advancing LLM-powered travel planning research and applications.

</details>


### [53] [Diagnosing LLM Arbitration Behavior over Pre-evidence Epistemic States in RAG-based Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01120)
*Yuxi Sun,Wenbo Shang,Wei Gao,Xin Huang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAVE诊断测试平台，用于评估RAG事实验证中LLM的**先验-上下文仲裁行为**（即模型在自身参数知识与检索证据冲突时的决策模式）。实验发现不同LLM的仲裁行为不可靠且高度依赖模型，并提出了基于JSD的轻量级测试时仲裁方法来提升事实可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG事实核查评估框架无法刻画LLM的**先验知识与检索证据之间的差异**，也无法衡量验证器如何在参数知识与上下文信号之间进行仲裁，因此需要一种能诊断这种仲裁行为的评估方法。

Method: 构建PAVE诊断测试平台：将LLM验证器在证据呈现前的状态划分为四种**认识论状态**（基于先验的正确性与置信度），然后在误导性证据/正确证据下评估其仲裁行为（坚持正确先验 vs. 纠正错误先验）。在7个LLM上进行实验，并提出基于JSD（JS散度）的测试时仲裁改进方法。

Result: 实验表明不同LLM的先验-上下文仲裁行为**不可靠且高度模型依赖**，说明实际RAG事实验证中选择合适的验证器至关重要。提出的JSD轻量级方法能在不修改底层模型的情况下提升事实可靠性，在多种LLM家族上取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: PAVE能有效诊断LLM在RAG事实验证中的先验-上下文仲裁问题，揭示了模型间显著的仲裁行为差异，而基于JSD的测试时方法提供了一种无需模型微调即可改善仲裁可靠性的实用方案。

Abstract: In RAG-based fact-checking, LLMs are increasingly used as verifiers to check given claims against retrieved evidence. Their parametric knowledge can induce pre-evidence tendencies that may conflict with the retrieved context, yet existing evaluation frameworks do not characterize such prior-context discrepancy or measure how verifiers arbitrate between parametric and contextual signals. We introduce \textsc{PAVE} (\emph{Prior-Aware Verifier Evaluation}), a diagnostic testbed that stratifies an LLM verifier into four epistemic states based on the correctness and confidence of its pre-evidence prior and evaluates its arbitration behavior on this new benchmark, i.e., whether it persists in correct prior under misleading evidence, and whether it corrects wrong prior when accurate evidence is provided.
