Alternative approaches, including predictive sampling and trajectory replay, aim to improve data efficiency but often remain insufficient and may introduce additional issues such as systematic bias or suboptimal constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Group Prioritized Off-Policy Optimization (POPO), a simple yet effective framework that fully exploits effective training batches without additional rollout overhead. POPO comprises two key components: prioritized group replay and decoupled off-policy optimization. The former replaces ineffective on-policy groups with effective off-policy groups via a recency-based replay mechanism that jointly considers sample quality and the degree of off-policiness. To further mitigate the off-policy gap, POPO employs decoupled importance sampling to correct off-policy bias while maintaining stable policy updates under consistent trust-region constraints. Empirical evaluations across diverse reasoning tasks, including mathematics, planning, and visual geometry, demonstrate that POPO substantially accelerates RL finetuning and achieves strong reasoning performance with significantly fewer rollouts.

</details>


### [127] [ChronosAD: Leveraging Time Series Foundation Models for Accurate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01300)
*Uzair Khan,Luigi Capogrosso,Francesco Biondani,Michele Magno,Franco Fummi,Francesco Setti,Marco Cristani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChronosAD提出了一种基于时间序列基础模型的特征提取器与自定义时序模块（BiLSTM+多头注意力）相结合的两阶段异常检测架构，在11个基准上平均AUC提升4.72%、AP提升6.60%，展现了跨领域的强大泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法难以跨数据集泛化，尤其在异常细微或依赖上下文时表现不佳。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段使用时间序列基础模型以零样本方式提取嵌入特征；第二阶段通过由双向长短期记忆网络（BiLSTM）和多头注意力组成的自定义时序模块（Temporal Block）精炼嵌入，捕获时序依赖并突出显著模式。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上，ChronosAD平均AUC提升4.72%，AP提升6.60%，覆盖工业、医疗、信息物理系统和汽车等领域。

Conclusion: ChronosAD仅需极少的任务特定微调即可实现跨领域的鲁棒泛化，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection is a crucial task in various domains, including finance, healthcare, and industry. However, existing methods often struggle to generalize across different datasets, especially when anomalies are subtle or context-dependent. To solve this issue, we introduce ChronosAD, a novel architecture for anomaly detection that uses a time series foundation model as a feature extractor. Specifically, it employs a two-stage pipeline: first, it uses the foundation model to extract embeddings for each time series in a zero-shot manner. Then, a custom-developed Temporal Block, composed of Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) and Multi-Head Attention, refines these embeddings to capture temporal dependencies and highlight salient patterns. Unlike previous approaches, our model requires minimal task-specific tuning and demonstrates robust generalization across a wide range of domains, including industrial, medical, cyber-physical, and automotive systems. Extensive experiments on 11 benchmarks show that ChronosAD outperforms existing methods by 4.72% in AUC and 6.60% in AP on average. The source code is available at https://github.com/intelligolabs/ChronosAD.

</details>


### [128] [When Hard Negatives Hurt: Bridging the Generative-Discriminative Gap in Hard Negative Synthesis for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01304)
*Zhicheng Zhang,Jiwei Tang,Kuicai Dong,Xiaopeng Li,Jieming Zhu,Jingyu Li,Qianhui Zhu,Fengyuan Lu,Wang Jiaheng,Gang Wang,Hai-Tao Zheng,Zhaocheng Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalNeg方法，通过因果反事实扰动构建负样本，并采用查询视角熵最大化训练策略，弥补大语言模型生成负样本与对比学习之间的生成-判别鸿沟，显著提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬负样本挖掘在检索训练中面临语料库限制、基于检索器分数而非诊断价值选择负样本、以及随检索器性能提升而出现的假阳性污染等固有限制。LLM合成负样本虽提供了无约束、有针对性、无假阳性风险的替代方案，但直接将其纳入对比学习往往导致检索性能下降。

Method: 提出CausalNeg框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于CoT引导的反事实扰动数据构造：将文档满足查询的原因分解为显式信息需求，然后有针对性地违反个别需求以构建具有可控、可解释硬度的负样本；(2) 训练时查询视角熵最大化：将生成负样本分散到相似度谱上，最小化来源身份与相似度分数之间的互信息以抑制捷径利用。

Result: 识别并形式化了LLM生成负样本与对比学习之间的生成-判别鸿沟，指出两个复合失效模式：判别无关生成（缺乏查询信息需求显式模型，生成通用或主题漂移文本）和源依赖捷径（分布特征使模型按来源而非相关性区分负样本，梯度漂移破坏优化）。

Conclusion: 通过因果反事实扰动和熵最大化策略，CausalNeg有效弥合了生成-判别鸿沟，为基于LLM合成的检索训练负样本提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Hard negative mining has become the dominant strategy for training retrievers, yet it faces intrinsic limitations: negatives are bounded by corpus availability, selected by retriever score rather than diagnostic value, and increasingly contaminated by false positives as the retriever improves. LLM-based synthesis offers a principled alternative, where negatives that are unconstrained, targeted, and free from false positive risk. But we show that naively incorporating generated negatives into contrastive learning often degrades retrieval performance. We identify and formalize the root cause as a generative-discriminative gap: LLM generation optimizes for fluent, plausible text, while contrastive learning demands strategic violations of relevance at the decision boundary. Our analysis reveals two compounding failure modes: discriminative-agnostic generation, where the LLM lacks an explicit model of query information needs and defaults to generic or topic-drifted text that provides no contrastive signal; and source-dependent shortcuts, where distributional artifacts enable the model to distinguish negatives by origin rather than relevance, causing gradient drift that actively corrupts optimization. To close this gap, we propose CausalNeg consisting of two main modules: (1) CoT-guided counterfactual perturbation for data construction: decomposes why a document satisfies a query into explicit information requirements, then surgically violates individual requirements to construct negatives with controlled, interpretable hardness. (2) Query-view entropy maximization during training: disperses generated negatives across the similarity spectrum, minimizing the mutual information between source identity and similarity scores to suppress shortcut exploitation. We make our code publicly available at https://github.com/mzhangzhicheng/CausalNeg.

</details>


### [129] [Efficient Exploration for Iterative Nash Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01382)
*Tianlong Nan,Xiaopeng Li,Christian Kroer,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线迭代式NLHF方法，指出标准迭代NLHF因缺乏显式探索会导致对KL正则化参数的指数依赖，并提出一种融合SFT正则化与对抗性策略探索的显式探索算法，实现了O(√T)的遗憾界，且在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上优于现有NLHF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基于奖励的偏好对齐方法在偏好循环、非传递等情况下存在局限，NLHF虽能解决该问题但缺乏可扩展的学习理论保证，现有迭代式NLHF方法虽易实现却无遗憾保证。

Method: （1）理论分析指出标准迭代NLHF存在对KL正则化参数的指数依赖；（2）提出显式探索的迭代NLHF算法，结合SFT正则化与对抗性策略探索；（3）利用极小极大oracle可进一步将遗憾界改进为O(log T)。

Result: 标准迭代NLHF存在指数依赖问题；所提算法实现O(√T)遗憾界且无指数依赖；引入oracle后可改进至O(log T)；在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上的实验验证了显式探索优于现有NLHF基线。

Conclusion: 揭示了NLHF学习中计算-统计的权衡，为可扩展的偏好对齐提供了理论保证与实践方法。

Abstract: Preference alignment is central to improving large language models, but standard reward-based formulations can be restrictive when human preferences are cyclic, non-transitive, or otherwise not representable by a scalar reward. Nash Learning from Human Feedback (NLHF) addresses this limitation by modeling alignment as a preference game and targeting a Nash equilibrium rather than a reward maximizer. However, the learning-theoretic foundations of scalable NLHF remain limited. Existing regret guarantees rely on oracle-based methods that estimate a general preference model and solve KL-regularized minimax problems, while iterative NLHF methods directly optimize policy-level preference losses and are easier to implement but lack regret guarantees. We study online iterative NLHF under general preference models and identify exploration as the key obstacle. First, we show that standard iterative NLHF can suffer an exponential dependence on the KL-regularization parameter, revealing that implicit exploration through policy updates is insufficient for controlling regret. Second, we propose an explicitly exploratory iterative NLHF algorithm that combines SFT-based regularization with adversarial policy exploration. The resulting method retains the direct policy optimization structure of iterative NLHF, avoids explicit preference model estimation, and achieves an $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret bound without an exponential dependence on the KL-regularization parameter. We show that the regret can be improved to $O(\log(T))$ with access to a minimax oracle, clarifying the computational-statistical tradeoff in learning general preference games. Finally, we instantiate our method for LLM fine-tuning and evaluate it on \texttt{Llama-3-8B-Instruct} across multiple benchmarks, where explicit exploration yields consistent improvements over existing NLHF baselines.

</details>


### [130] [Truthful AI Advisors: A Pre-Specified Benchmark for Large Language Model Honesty Under Preference Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01456)
*Hamidreza Hasani Balyani,Seyed Pouyan Mousavi Davoudi,Alireza Amiri-Margavi,Amin Gholami Davodi,Arshia Gharagozlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Crawford-Sobel廉价交谈模型基准测试，发现四种指令微调大语言模型在利益冲突下过度诚实（信息揭示量比最优均衡高1.8-4.2倍），呈现近乎完全揭示而非理论预测的粗糙分区，且这一现象仅在接受者读取发送者明确数值时显现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在作为推荐系统、销售助手等非对齐目标场景中，面对诚实与自身利益冲突时的行为，检验其是否如理论预测那样进行策略性信息扭曲。

Method: 基于Crawford-Sobel廉价交谈模型设计基准测试：设置5个偏差水平（b∈{0.01,0.04,0.08,0.12}加上零偏差基线）、3种提示框架、固定低温设置、每单元200个状态，共12,000次发送者调用。测试四种指令微调模型（GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite, Llama-3.3-70B），并使用解码器消融实验验证结果来源。

Result: 所有四种模型的信息揭示量远超最信息性均衡的理论值（归一化互信息0.78-0.94 vs 理论最优0.18-0.53），呈现近乎完全揭示的模式，而非理论预测的粗糙分区。信息量随偏差增大而下降但远未达到策略最优；收益最大化 vs 诚实框架影响微乎其微；消融实验表明该现象仅在接受者读取发送者明确数值时才可复现。

Conclusion: 当前指令微调大语言模型在利益冲突下表现出过度诚实（线性夸大偏差），而非策略性信息扭曲，这一行为偏离了经典博弈论预测的最优策略，揭示了模型对齐中的一个系统性偏差。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as advisors whose objective is not aligned with the user's: recommenders optimize for engagement, sales assistants for purchases, negotiation agents for concessions. Whether such advisors stay truthful when honesty conflicts with their own payoff is a core alignment-evaluation question. We turn the canonical Crawford-Sobel cheap-talk model into a pre-specified benchmark for LLM honesty under preference misalignment. Cheap-talk theory predicts neither full revelation nor silence but coarse monotone partitions, with fewer informative intervals as preference conflict grows. A sender observes a state omega in [0,1], wants the receiver's action near omega+b, and sends one costless message to a receiver whose ideal action is omega. The design uses 5 bias levels, 3 prompt frames, a fixed low-temperature setting, and 200 states per cell: 12,000 sender calls. For the positive-bias grid b in {0.01,0.04,0.08,0.12} the exact most-informative partition sizes are 7,4,3,2, with oracle normalized mutual information 0.5294, 0.3268, 0.2205, 0.1829. Running the full design on four instruction-tuned models (GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite, Llama-3.3-70B), we find all four over-reveal relative to the most-informative equilibrium by 1.8 to 4.2x: normalized mutual information stays at 0.78-0.94 where the oracle prescribes 0.18-0.53. Informativeness declines with bias as predicted but never approaches the strategic optimum; rather than coarse partitions, models show near-full revelation with a constant upward offset tracking their bias (linear exaggeration). Payoff-maximizing versus honesty framing has negligible effect. A decoder ablation shows the finding is recoverable only when the receiver reads the sender's stated number: an embedding-only decoder mis-reads the same data as near-babbling.

</details>


### [131] [Genotype-Conditioned Molecular Generation via Evidence-Grounded Multi-Objective Latent Perturbation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01461)
*Brenda Nogueira,Gisela A. Gonzalez-Montiel,Nitesh V. Chawla,Nuno Moniz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜空间优化的生成式方法，通过在预训练的基因型到药物扩散模型上引入可学习的扰动，利用梯度上升最大化药物敏感性、类药性和合成可及性的复合奖励，并借助多智能体LLM管线评估机制一致性，在15个癌细胞系上实现了优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因型条件生成模型缺乏对药物敏感性、可合成性和机制性结合合理性的联合显式优化，难以有效应对肿瘤异质性和分子靶点缺失带来的个性化药物发现挑战。

Method: 在预训练的基因型到药物扩散模型的分子潜空间上引入可学习的扰动，通过梯度上升优化一个包含AUC（药物敏感性）、QED（类药性）和SAS（合成可及性）的复合奖励函数；同时利用基于扩散模型注意力机制的多智能体LLM管线评估机制一致性合理性。

Result: 在三个留出评估集的15个癌细胞系上，该方法在药物敏感性、类药性、合成可及性和化学有效性方面均一致且显著优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: 将实验衍生的癌细胞系数据和已验证的药理学信号融入奖励设计与评估，能有效将候选分子生成锚定在真实临床证据上，为个性化抗癌药物发现提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Developing effective anticancer therapeutics remains challenging due to tumor heterogeneity and the absence of well-defined molecular targets across cancer subtypes. Generative models conditioned on cancer genotypes offer a promising avenue for personalized drug discovery, yet existing approaches lack explicit optimization for simultaneous sensitivity, synthesizability, and mechanistic binding plausibility. We present a latent-space optimization approach for a pretrained genotype-to-drug diffusion model, introducing a learnable perturbation over the molecular latent space optimized via gradient ascent to maximize a composite reward combining predicted drug sensitivity (AUC), drug-likeness (QED), and synthetic accessibility (SAS). Critically, biological realism is enforced by grounding both reward design and evaluation in experimentally-derived cancer cell line data and validated pharmacologic signals, anchoring candidate generation in real-world clinical evidence. Mechanistic consistency plausibility is further assessed by a multi-agent LLM pipeline grounded in the diffusion model's attention mechanism. Experiments across 15 cancer cell lines from three held-out evaluation sets demonstrate consistent and noticeable improvements over competing baselines in sensitivity, drug-likeness, synthesizability, and chemical validity.

</details>


### [132] [ATLAS: Agentic Test-time Learning-to-Allocate Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01667)
*Peijia Qin,Qi Cao,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS，一种智能体化测试时扩展框架，让LLM编排器端到端控制推理过程中的计算资源分配（是否收集更多证据、何时停止、如何综合答案），通过单一的"explore"动作实现，在四个基准上达到SOTA性能且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法中，计算预算、精炼循环、评分规则或搜索策略等编排方式均由人工设计固定，模型只负责求解而不负责编排，缺乏自适应性和灵活性。

Method: ATLAS框架中，LLM编排器通过单一的"explore"动作（可指定求解器、推理努力程度或提示策略）自主决定何时收集更多证据、何时停止以及如何综合最终答案，实现端到端的控制循环。ATLAS-MM扩展版本将求解器选择作为额外动作维度。

Result: 在Claude Sonnet 4.6骨干下：HLE-Verified 56.00%，LiveCodeBench 82.29%，GPQA-Diamond 85.75%，BabyVision 23.71%，且API调用量远少于固定工作流基线。ATLAS-MM进一步将HLE-Verified提升至60.00%，LiveCodeBench提升至85.63%。

Conclusion: 让LLM编排器自主管理测试时计算资源分配是有效且高效的；状态化的证据管理对性能提升至关重要，替换为独立整合器反而导致性能下降。

Abstract: Test-time scaling has become a major way to improve large language model reasoning, but its orchestration has remained designer-engineered: a fixed sample budget, a fixed refinement loop, a fixed scoring rule, or a fixed search policy decides how compute is spent, leaving the model in charge of solving but not of orchestration. We introduce ATLAS, an agentic test-time scaling framework in which an LLM orchestrator owns the control loop end-to-end. Through a single action, explore, which dispatches a fresh independent solver on the original problem, the orchestrator decides whether to gather more evidence, when to stop, and how to synthesize the final answer; the action space is extensible, with each explore call optionally specifying solver, reasoning effort, or prompting strategy. We evaluate ATLAS on four benchmarks covering scientific question answering, code generation, and multimodal reasoning under a Claude Sonnet 4.6 backbone, where it reaches 56.00% on HLE-Verified, 82.29% on LiveCodeBench, 85.75% on GPQA-Diamond, and 23.71% on BabyVision while using far fewer API calls than fixed-workflow baselines. A multi-model extension, ATLAS-MM, that exposes solver choice as an additional action dimension further improves HLE-Verified to 60.00% and LiveCodeBench to 85.63%, with consistent gains on GPQA-Diamond and BabyVision. Ablations replacing the orchestrator's direct synthesis with a separate integrator degrade or fail to improve accuracy on three of four benchmarks, consistent with the role of stateful evidence management in producing the gains.

</details>


### [133] [RDA: Reward Design Agent for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01672)
*Hojoon Lee,Ajay Subramanian,Ben Abbatematteo,Vijay Veerabadran,Pedro Matias,Karl Ridgeway,Nitin Kamra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出奖励设计智能体（RDA），一种基于VLM的智能体框架，通过视觉评估轨迹、总结失败模式并迭代修正奖励函数，使机器人策略更好地与任务指令对齐。在12个桌面操作和4个全身操作任务上，RDA显著提升了指令对齐度，同时保持了与基线相当的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习的奖励函数需要手工设计，且基于LLM的自动奖励设计方法（如Eureka）仅依赖成功率等粗粒度反馈信号，缺乏对学习到的行为的语义理解，导致训练出的策略虽然达到最终目标，却经常与任务指令对齐不良。

Method: RDA是一种基于VLM的智能体框架，核心流程包括：任务分解、视觉评估轨迹、总结失败模式、迭代修正奖励代码，从而注入语义理解到奖励设计过程中。

Result: 在ManiSkill的12个桌面操作任务和HumanoidBench的4个全身操作任务上，RDA训练出的策略在指令对齐度上显著优于其他基线方法，同时任务成功率与基线相当。

Conclusion: 通过引入VLM的视觉语义理解能力，RDA能够更有效地将任务指令的语义信息注入奖励函数设计，从而获得与人类意图更一致的机器人行为，为自动化奖励设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has enabled the acquisition of impressive robotic skills, but typically requires hand-crafted reward functions that are slow to design and difficult to align with human intentions. Recent work, such as Eureka, automates reward design by using an LLM to iteratively generate and refine reward code from task descriptions. However, they rely on coarse feedback signals such as success rate, which provide little semantic insight into the learned behavior. As a result, their trained policies achieve the final goal but are frequently poorly aligned with task instructions. We introduce the Reward Design Agent (RDA), a VLM-based agentic framework that injects semantic understanding into reward design. RDA decomposes tasks, visually evaluates trajectories, summarizes failure modes, and iteratively revises reward code to better align with task instructions. Across 12 tabletop manipulation tasks from ManiSkill and 4 whole-body manipulation tasks from HumanoidBench, RDA produces policies substantially more instruction-aligned than those of other baselines, while achieving comparable task success rates. Videos and the generated reward code are available on https://nitinkamra1992.github.io/reward-design-agent.

</details>


### [134] [Shortcut to Nowhere: Demystifying Deep Spurious Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01723)
*Guanrong Xu,Jessica Li,Hao Wang,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了深度虚假回归（DSR）问题，即连续预测中属性与标签存在虚假相关，并提出在标签空间和特征空间中利用虚假属性的相似性进行校准的方法，在多个真实世界数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假相关性研究主要聚焦分类任务，但许多真实场景需要连续预测（回归），其中不存在明确的类别边界或离散的组-标签对。回归任务中属性与标签的虚假相关会导致测试时灾难性失败，因此需要专门针对连续预测的虚假相关性研究。

Method: 利用虚假属性在标签空间和特征空间中的相似性，同时校准跨属性的标签分布和学习的特征分布，从而处理邻近目标及相关组别，实现对所有属性-标签组合的泛化。

Result: 在涵盖计算机视觉、环境感知和大语言模型回归的真实世界DSR数据集上，所提策略取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: 本文填补了连续预测中虚假相关性研究的基准和技术空白。

Abstract: Real-world regression often exhibits shortcuts: attributes that are spuriously correlated with continuous targets in training, yet unreliable under deployment shifts; regressing targets using such shortcuts may fail catastrophically at test time. Existing studies on spurious correlations focus primarily on classification, where labels are categorical and groups are naturally defined. However, many real-world tasks require continuous prediction, where hard label boundaries or discrete group-label pairs do not exist. We define Deep Spurious Regression (DSR) as learning from regression data with attribute-label confounding, addressing continuous spurious correlations, and generalizing to all attribute-label combinations at test time. Motivated by the intrinsic difference between classification and regression shortcuts, we propose to exploit the similarity among spurious attributes in both label and feature spaces, thereby accounting for nearby targets and related groups while calibrating both label and learned feature distributions across attributes. Extensive experiments on common real-world DSR datasets that span computer vision, environmental sensing, and large language model (LLM) regression verify the superior performance of our strategies. Our work fills the gap in benchmarks and techniques for studying spurious correlations in continuous prediction.

</details>


### [135] [Adaptive Auto-Harness: Sustained Self-Improvement for Agentic System Deployment on Open-Ended Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01770)
*Zewen Liu,Zhan Shi,Yisi Sang,Bing He,Minhua Lin,Tianxin Wei,Dakuo Wang,Benoit Dumoulin,Wei Jin,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Adaptive Auto-Harness框架，针对开放端任务流中传统自动装配系统性能退化问题，通过状态化多智能体进化器、装配树与路由、人工干预钩子三种机制，在多个任务流上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动装配系统（如A-Evolve、GEPA、Meta-Harness）在固定离线基准上表现良好，但实际部署面对开放端任务流时（历史无限增长、任务异构、分布漂移），单次密集更新的装配会变得脆弱，准确率早期达峰后持续下降，因此需要支持任务级自适应的持续装配构建方法。

Method: 将到oracle装配的差距分解为进化损失和适应损失；系统包含三个核心组件：1) 状态化多智能体进化器，2) 带求解时路由的装配树，3) 人工干预钩子（用于历史缺乏足够信号的场景）。

Result: 在预测市场、安全竞赛、事件预测三个任务流上，Adaptive Auto-Harness优于五种现有自动装配基线；消融实验归因了性能提升来自更好的装配构建、路由或定向人工干预。

Conclusion: 面向开放端任务流的自适应装配框架有效解决了传统方法的性能退化问题，通过分解损失、多智能体进化、装配树路由和人工干预机制实现了持续的task-wise适应。

Abstract: Auto-harness systems such as A-Evolve, GEPA, and Meta-Harness improve LLM agents by optimizing prompts, skills, tools, memories, and supporting infrastructure from execution feedback, but they are typically evaluated on fixed offline benchmarks. Real deployments instead present open-ended task streams: histories grow without a fixed endpoint, heterogeneous tasks require different harnesses, and problem distributions shift over time. These challenges make a single repeatedly and densely updated harness brittle, causing performance degradation as accuracy peaks early and then declines. This motivates sustained harness construction with task-wise adaptation. We introduce Adaptive Auto-Harness, a framework and system for such streams. The framework decomposes the gap to an oracle harness into evolution loss and adaptation loss. The system addresses these losses with a stateful multi-agent evolver, a harness tree with solve-time routing, and human-steering hooks for cases where history lacks the needed signal. Across prediction-market, security-competition, and event-forecasting streams, Adaptive Auto-Harness outperforms five existing auto-harness baselines and ablations attribute gains to better construction, routing, or targeted human steering. Code is available in https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/AdaptiveHarness .

</details>


### [136] [Learning Implicit Bias in Generative Spaces for Accelerating Protein Dynamics Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01833)
*Kaihui Cheng,Zhiqiang Cai,Wenkai Xiang,Zhihang Hu,Siyu Zhu,Tzuhsiung Yang,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于历史依赖偏差的生成式蛋白质动力学模拟方法，通过在预训练仿真器中引入隐式偏置和分数细化步骤，提升生成长度多样性并加速稀有状态覆盖，实验显示多样性提升35%，零样本蛋白质中覆盖速度提升最高37倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质动力学生成式仿真器虽能高效生成轨迹，但受限于训练分布，在长期外推时倾向于重复已知状态而难以到达稀有状态，需要一种增强采样方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 在冻结的预训练仿真器生成空间中引入隐式的历史依赖偏置：使用历史感知的分数估计器添加距离加权偏置，引导逆向时间采样远离已生成结构；通过环境支持项正则化；并采用基于分数的细化步骤将漂移样本重新投影到数据流形上以保持长期结构有效性。

Result: 在DynamicPDB-80数据集上多样性提升35%；在12个零样本Fast-Folding蛋白质上，仅使用学习偏置即可比无偏仿真器快~15倍达到相同覆盖，结合细化步骤可快~37倍，且覆盖的低能状态数量提升~3倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了生成式仿真器在长期外推中陷入已知状态的局限，显著提升了采样多样性和稀有状态发现效率，为增强采样与生成式模型的结合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative emulators of protein dynamics produce plausible trajectories at a fraction of the cost of molecular dynamics, but they inherit their training distribution and tend to revisit known states rather than reach rare ones under long-horizon extrapolation. Inspired by classical enhanced sampling, we introduce an implicit, history-dependent bias in the generative space of a pretrained emulator. Specifically, a history-aware score estimator augments the frozen emulator with a distance-weighted bias that steers reverse-time sampling away from previously generated structures, regularized by an environment-support term. To preserve structural validity at long horizons, a score-based refinement step re-projects drifted samples onto the data manifold using the frozen emulator. Our experiments demonstrate that the method (i) raises diversity by $35\%$ on DynamicPDB-80; (ii) on $12$ zero-shot Fast-Folding proteins, the learned bias alone reaches the unbiased emulator's coverage up to ${\sim}15\times$ faster, and pairing it with refinement reaches the coverage up to ${\sim}37\times$ faster while covering ${\sim}3\times$ as many low-energy states. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [137] [G2LoRA: Gradient Orthogonal Low-Rank Adaptation Framework for Graph Continual Learning on Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01873)
*Yuhan Wang,Yibo Ding,Yutong Ye,Mufan Zhao,Wenbo Zhang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出G2LoRA框架，解决LLM-as-Aligner模型在图属性文本数据上的灾难性遗忘问题，通过统一优化目标、类别感知梯度投影和跨模态梯度协调，在持续学习场景中实现优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-Aligner模型在流式任务顺序微调时存在严重的灾难性遗忘问题，参数高效微调虽能部分缓解，但不足以解决任务干扰和无效知识迁移。

Method: G2LoRA包含三个核心设计：(1) 统一图-文本对齐目标，将节点、链接、图级任务统一为单一对齐目标；(2) 类别感知梯度投影，在结构化子空间中解决冲突更新并实现有条件反向迁移；(3) 梯度幅度调制，协调图编码器和文本编码器之间的更新速率以防止跨模态漂移。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，G2LoRA在不同骨干架构下持续优于强基线方法，展现出卓越的持续学习性能和可迁移性。

Conclusion: G2LoRA有效缓解了LLM-as-Aligner在图文属性图持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，实现了正向知识迁移和跨模态一致性维持。

Abstract: LLM-as-Aligner has emerged as a prevalent pre-training paradigm for Text-Attributed Graphs(TAGS), aligning graph and text modalities into a shared embedding space via CLIP-style contrastive learning. While effective on individual downstream tasks, we observe severe catastrophic forgetting when such models are sequentially fine-tuned on streaming tasks. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning alleviates forgetting to some extent, it remains insufficient to resolve task interference and ineffective knowledge transfer. In this work, we study graph continual learning for LLM-as-Aligner models on TAGs, with the goal of mitigating interference while promoting positive transfer across tasks. This setting introduces two fundamental challenges: (1) heterogeneous downstream tasks induce shifting optimization objectives, hindering unified fine-tuning; and (2) graph and text encoders exhibit different sensitivities to adaptation, making uncoordinated updates prone to misalignment. To address these challenges, we propose G2LoRA, a continual learning framework for TAGs. G2LoRA unifies node-, link-, and graph-level tasks under a single graph--text alignment objective, and enables consistent optimization across domain/class/task incremental modes. To reduce task interference while encouraging positive transfer, G2LoRA performs category-aware gradient projection in structured subspaces, resolving conflicting updates and enabling conditional backward transfer to balance forward and backward knowledge flow. To further prevent cross-modal drift, G2LoRA introduces gradient magnitude modulation to coordinate update rates between graph and text encoders. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that G2LoRA consistently outperforms strong baselines across different backbone architectures, achieving superior continual performance and transferability.

</details>


### [138] [Planar Symmetric Pattern Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02073)
*Ning Lin,Luxi Chen,Huaguan Chen,Jiacheng Cen,Chongxuan Li,Wenbing Huang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对称化框架，可将任意2D连续表示转换为具有平面群对称性且保持连续性的表示，并通过多项设计任务验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D连续表示难以在不破坏连续性的前提下施加平面群对称性，因为非反射群元素的变换可能导致不连续。

Method: 提出一个对称化框架，给出满足对称性且保持连续的数学表示形式，证明其逼近能力，并详细阐述构建方法。

Result: 在图案设计、剪纸设计、风格化拓扑设计和材料设计四项任务中实现了有效的对称性控制。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效控制任意平面群的对称性，并具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Generating objects with specific symmetries is essential in various real-world scenarios. However, adapting existing 2D continuous representations to enforce planar group symmetry remains a challenge, as the transformation of non-reflective group elements may disrupt continuity. To overcome this limitation, we propose a symmetrization framework for arbitrary planar groups. Our method transforms any 2D continuous representation into a symmetric one while preserving continuity. We provide the mathematical formulation of this representation, demonstrate its approximation capability for symmetric functions, and detail the construction methodology. We validate our approach through three visual design tasks (pattern design, paper-cutting design and stylized topology design) and one material design task. Experiments confirm that our representation enables effective symmetry control and demonstrate its broader applicability.

</details>


### [139] [When Tabular Foundation Models Transfer Across Modalities: A Systematic Evaluation Across 95 Datasets, 7 Modalities, and Two Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02106)
*Julien Lafrance*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合等角紧框架（ETF）预处理和表格基础模型的通用分类流水线（TabICL），在95个数据集（涵盖七种模态）上评估，相比强轻量级基线具有竞争性且速度快4-200倍，并提供了实际部署指南（包括校准、停止训练策略及适用场景判断）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏一个跨模态的、轻量且快速的通用分类流水线，且常依赖繁重的微调。作者希望构建一个固定比较对象、在冻结特征上统一评估的流水线，并解决其实际部署中的校准和训练停止等问题。

Method: 将ETF预处理层与基于上下文的表格基础模型结合，形成一个统一的分类流水线。数据先映射为固定向量，经ETF预处理后输入上下文分类器。在95个数据集（视觉、音频、语音、文本、分子、时间序列、表格）上评估，与最强轻量级调优基线在相同冻结特征上比较。

Result: 流水线在冻结特征上与强轻量级调优基线具有广泛竞争性；虽然不如高度调优或专门化模型，但差距很小；推理速度比完全骨干调优快4-200倍；ETF预处理会破坏校准，但后处理缩放可恢复校准概率。

Conclusion: 提出了一个跨模态通用、快速且实用的分类流水线，提供了实际部署建议（ETF预处理应用时机、无验证集停止训练、上下文分类器设置、校准恢复），并指出了不适用场景及预判方法。

Abstract: We present a single classification pipeline that combines an Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) preprocessing stage with a tabular foundation model for in-context inference, applied identically across modalities once data is mapped to fixed vector representations. We evaluate it on 95 datasets spanning seven signal modalities -- vision, audio, speech, text, molecular, time-series, and tabular. The main methodological contribution is to fix the comparison object: throughout the paper, performance is judged against the strongest lightweight tuned baseline on the same frozen features, while oracle selection, deployed selection, and specialized fine-tuning are reported separately.
  The pipeline is broadly competitive with strong lightweight tuned baselines on the same frozen features. It does not match the very best specialized models or heavily tuned pipelines on every task, but it stays close, and it runs much faster -- typically 4 to 200 times faster than full backbone fine-tuning, often at comparable quality.
  We describe how to deploy the pipeline in practice: when to apply ETF preprocessing, how to stop its training without a validation split, how to set up the in-context classifier, and how to calibrate the resulting probabilities. The calibration step is non-cosmetic: TabICL produces well-calibrated probabilities by construction, ETF preprocessing initially disrupts that calibration, and the post-hoc rescaling restores it -- yielding a per-prediction confidence signal that practitioners can use as a trust threshold for confidence-gated deployment. We also report where the pipeline should not be expected to help, and how to identify those cases in advance.

</details>


### [140] [Local Preferential Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02351)
*Johanna Menn,Miriam Kober,Paul Brunzema,David Stenger,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部偏好贝叶斯优化方法，将置信域和导数信息局部搜索策略扩展到偏好反馈场景，有效解决高维优化问题，显著降低累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好贝叶斯优化方法在处理高维问题时效率低下，因为它们依赖全局搜索策略，而实际应用中（如策略搜索）常面临高维、陡峭最优值的复杂景观，亟需高效的局部优化方法。

Method: 开发了局部PBO方法家族，将高维BO中的置信域方法和导数信息局部搜索扩展到成对偏好反馈场景，利用拉普拉斯近似GP后验的一阶和二阶导数指导搜索。

Result: 在GP样本路径、标准优化基准函数和策略搜索任务上的基准测试表明，局部PBO方法在高维和具有陡峭最优值的复杂景观中尤其有效，相比全局偏好基线方法能大幅降低累积遗憾。

Conclusion: 局部PBO方法在处理高维偏好优化问题上具有显著优势，尤其适用于策略搜索等实际任务，为偏好贝叶斯优化提供了更高效的高维解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular and effective approach for tuning expensive, noisy experiments, but requires the formulation of an explicit objective function. Preferential BO (PBO) removes this requirement by learning from pairwise human feedback, yet existing methods struggle to efficiently optimize beyond low- and medium-dimensional problems due to their global search approaches. We address this limitation by developing a family of local PBO methods that transfer key ideas from high-dimensional BO to the preferential setting. In particular, we introduce local PBO methods which adapt trust-region and derivative-informed local search to pairwise preference feedback, where the latter exploits first- and second-order derivatives of the Laplace-approximated GP posterior. Our benchmark on GP sample paths, standard optimization benchmark functions, and policy-search tasks shows that local PBO methods are especially effective in high-dimensional and complex landscapes with steep optima. Compared with global preference-based baselines, they can substantially reduce cumulative regret, making them particularly useful for real-world preference-based optimization tasks such as policy search.

</details>


### [141] [Policy and World Modeling Co-Training for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02388)
*Ning Lu,Baijiong Lin,Shengcai Liu,Jiahao Wu,Haoze Lv,Yanbin Wei,Lingting Zhu,Shengju Qian,Xin Wang,Ying-Cong Chen,Qi Wang,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PaW框架通过在在线策略RL的rollout中利用动作-下一观测对作为世界模型监督信号，在不改变推理方式的情况下同时训练策略和世界模型，显著提升了语言智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能指导LLM智能体选择高奖励动作，但对动作如何影响环境缺乏监督；世界模型可弥补这一缺陷，但现有方法需要独立模拟器、额外训练阶段或推理计算开销。

Method: 提出PaW框架，在RL策略训练的同时添加辅助世界模型监督信号，包含三个组件：基于动作熵的WM数据选择、容忍噪声的WM损失函数、奖励自适应的损失平衡机制。

Result: 在三个智能体任务基准上，PaW在不同模型和RL算法下均一致优于强RL基线。

Conclusion: 标准RL rollouts可作为语言智能体训练中实用的世界模型监督信号来源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) improves large language model (LLM) agents by teaching them which actions lead to high rewards, but provides little supervision on what those actions do to the environment. World modeling (WM) can fill this gap, yet existing approaches often require separate simulators, extra training stages, or additional inference-time computation. We observe that on-policy RL rollouts already contain the needed signal: each transition pairs an action with its resulting next observation. Based on this observation, we propose PaW, a Policy and World modeling co-training framework that adds auxiliary WM supervision to the same policy during RL, without changing the inference paradigm. To make auxiliary WM supervision informative and stable, PaW introduces three components: action-entropy-based WM data selection, noise-tolerant WM loss, and reward-adaptive loss balancing. Experiments on three agentic task benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong RL baselines across models and RL algorithms. These results suggest that standard RL rollouts are a practical source of WM supervision for language-agent training.

</details>
